Abstract: Continual pre-training of large language models must acquire new information without erasing old knowledge. Existing replay methods often choose a global old/new mixture and sample uniformly, ignoring that examples differ in how quickly they are forgotten. We formulate continual pre-training as adaptive review scheduling: the training loop should decide not only how much history to replay, but which examples should return at each step. We introduce Spaced Repetition Training (SRT), a continual learning framework inspired by cognitive science, which schedules sample-rehearsal using the SuperMemo-2 (SM-2) algorithm. SRT maintains per-example review state, maps per-example perplexity to a recall-quality signal, and schedules historical examples for retention and new examples for consolidation while leaving the model, objective, and optimizer unchanged. On temporally separated Wikipedia and code corpora, SRT improves the stability-plasticity trade-off, recovering 5 to 37 percentage points of old-knowledge accuracy lost by naive continual pre-training across model scales while preserving or improving new-knowledge acquisition. At larger scale, SRT preserves broad benchmark performance that naive continual pre-training and uniform replay substantially degrade. Experiments with vision and tabular data further suggest that the scheduling principle extends beyond language when paired with an appropriate recall signal.

</details>


### [38] [Quantifying Risk Under Evolving Uncertainty: Belief-Dependent Robustness for Safe Sequential Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17574)
*Deep Kumar Ganguly,Jan Kretinsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RATTL框架，通过贝叶斯后验构建Wasserstein模糊集，使智能体的谨慎程度随认知不确定性自适应调整，实现从最坏情况鲁棒到风险中性的连续过渡，并证明其值介于鲁棒与最优之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在未知环境中学习时，过度保守会损害效率，过度冒险则可能带来风险，需要一种根据当前认知不确定性动态调整谨慎程度的方法。

Method: 基于贝叶斯后验构造Wasserstein模糊集，半径由后验熵决定，利用Entropic Value-at-Risk对偶性设计规划问题，并证明在瞬态和紧致条件下的适定性。

Result: RATTL的值介于无信息鲁棒值与完全信息最优值之间，且随后验集中差距消失；在二元风险实例中退化为后验熵设定的CVaR，示例显示智能体延迟高效行动直至识别阈值。

Conclusion: RATTL为不确定环境中的智能体（包括LLM系统）提供了可解释的实时安全决策机制，平衡鲁棒性与最优性。

Abstract: How cautious should an agent be while it is still learning its environment? We propose RATTL (Risk-Adversarial Total-Reward Learning), which ties caution to epistemic uncertainty: the agent holds a Bayesian posterior over unknown dynamics and plans against a Wasserstein ambiguity set whose radius is a monotone function of that posterior. The radius contracts with evidence, so behaviour interpolates continuously between worst-case robustness and risk-neutral total-reward maximization. The design follows the duality underlying the Entropic Value-at-Risk, which converts the choice of a risk level into the choice of an ambiguity radius. We show the resulting planning problem is well posed under transience and compactness conditions, and prove a Safety Sandwich: the RATTL value lies between the uninformed robust value and the full- knowledge optimum, with a gap that vanishes as the posterior concentrates. In a canonical binary-hazard instance, the induced criterion reduces to Conditional Value-at-Risk at a level set by the posterior entropy. A worked example shows the agent deferring the efficient action until a sharp identification threshold. RATTL targets runtime safety for agents, including LLM-based systems, acting under uncertainty.

</details>


### [39] [TRUSS: Towards Task-Reliable and User-Safe Automated Agent Skill Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17588)
*Zhibo Zhang,Zhen Ouyang,Ling Shi,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRUSS框架，通过静态检查和动态执行环境生成既高效又安全的Agent技能，实验表明其能显著提升任务完成率并消除安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动生成Agent技能的方法仅评估最终任务结果，无法揭示实际执行的动作及其副作用，导致功能和安全问题未被发现。

Method: TRUSS框架首先对技能工件进行静态检查（功能声明与证据比对，九项安全属性评估），然后通过影子代理在可控执行环境中运行，利用工具代理记录执行轨迹，并将失败与违规映射回具体内容以指导迭代修复。

Result: 在多个基准上，漏洞检测精确率和召回率均为100%，修复后攻击成功率显著下降且无回归，技能生成任务效率从17.11%提升至52.94%，安全率提升至100%。

Conclusion: 执行证据能够揭示工件检查遗漏的行为失败，并能引导技能生成实现功能与安全的共同验证。

Abstract: Agent Skills package reusable natural language procedures with executable resources, enabling software agents to acquire task specific capabilities without model adaptation. Automatically generating such Skills can improve task performance, yet evaluating a candidate solely from its artifact or final task outcome leaves unresolved which actions the equipped agent will perform and which side effects those actions will produce. We present TRUSS, an evidence guided framework for generating functionally effective and safety reliable Agent Skills. TRUSS first inspects functional claims against source and domain evidence while evaluating the complete artifact under nine predefined safety properties. Candidates admitted by this static gate are loaded by a shadow agent inside a Controllable Execution Environment, where brokered tools expose requested actions to policy enforcement and record their results as provenance preserving execution traces. Functional failures and property violations are linked back to the responsible Skill content and used to guide iterative refinement.
  We evaluate TRUSS on 168 SkillInject artifacts, 155 SkillSafetyBench cases, and all 187 tasks in SkillGenBench. TRUSS achieves 100.00\% precision and recall in vulnerability detection. Repair reduces attack success from 38.71\% to 19.35\% with GPT 5.5 and from 46.45\% to 29.68\% with GPT 5.4, with zero attack regression. For Skill generation, TRUSS raises task effectiveness from 17.11\% without Skills to 52.94\%, while increasing the benchmark Security rate from 50.80\% to 100.00\%. These results show that execution evidence can expose behavioral failures missed by artifact inspection and can guide Skill generation toward jointly verified functional and safety outcomes.

</details>

*Wonguk Cho,Kyubyung Chae,Tribhuvanesh Orekondy,Sunghyun Park,Hyoungwoo Park,Jeongho Kim,Arash Behboodi,Kyuwoong Hwang,Sungrack Yun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MoNe是一种轻量级模块化神经记忆，可附加到任意冻结的预训练Transformer上，实现无需重训练的长上下文推理。它通过测试时学习快速权重神经记忆网络，将推理成本与上下文长度解耦，实现O(N)预处理和O(1)查询成本，在128K tokens时相比ICL减少约80%的计算和显存开销，并在长上下文基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer模型在处理长上下文时，推理成本随序列长度增长而增加，且微调或扩展窗口需要大量资源。作者旨在设计一种轻量级、模块化的记忆机制，在不重训练模型的情况下，使冻结的预训练Transformer能高效处理远超其原生窗口的上下文。

Method: MoNe采用两阶段设计：预处理阶段将上下文划分为固定大小的段，通过测试时学习（test-time learning）训练快速权重神经记忆网络，更新仅局部化到特定层；推理阶段，记忆仅从查询令牌生成键和值，无需重新读取上下文令牌。这种设计将推理成本与上下文长度解耦，实现O(N)预处理和O(1)查询成本，峰值GPU内存不随N增长。

Result: 在128K tokens时，MoNe相比ICL减少约80%的计算和峰值GPU内存开销，仅增加6.4%的参数。在RULER基准（如针在干草堆和单词提取）上，MoNe在远超骨干网络原生窗口的上下文长度上保持强性能，而ICL性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: MoNe展示了通过轻量级模块化记忆实现高效长上下文推理的可行性，无需重训练模型，且能泛化到极端长度，为长上下文处理提供了新方向。

Abstract: We present MoNe, a lightweight modular neural memory that attaches to any frozen pretrained Transformer to enable long-context inference without retraining. MoNe reads context in fixed-size segments via test-time learning of fast-weight neural memory networks with layer-localized gradient updates; at inference, the memory generates keys and values from the query tokens alone, with no context tokens re-read. This two-phase design decouples inference cost from context length, achieving $O(N)$ preprocessing and $O(1)$ query cost with peak GPU memory that does not grow with $N$. At 128K tokens, MoNe reduces both compute and peak GPU memory by approximately 80% compared to ICL with only 6.4% parameter overhead. MoNe generalizes to context lengths far beyond the backbone's native window, achieving strong performance on needle-in-a-haystack and word extraction benchmarks from RULER, where ICL degrades sharply.

</details>


### [41] [Validated Adaptation for Aerial Crowd Monitoring at Mass Gathering Scale: A Deployment Protocol, a Severity Law, and a Diagnostic for Label-Free Drone Crowd Counting, Toward the FIFA World Cup 2034 (Saudi Arabia)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17625)
*AlAnoud AllGhayth,AlJawharh AlOtaibi,Jude AlSubaie*

Main category: cs.AI

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沙特将主办2034年世界杯且已有朝觐人群管理经验，无人机计数需在无标签、训练语料外的高精度场景中运行，并提前预警危险人流，防止踩踏发生。

Method: 采用无标签域自适应技术，结合通量风险评估模块，通过525次受控实验、全分辨率语料研究、五组消融实验和五条件安全联锁评估，系统分析自适应方法在四种损坏和五种严重度下的表现，并建立严重度定律和稳定性预算。

Result: 无标签自适应恢复了31-49%的偏移误差，最强方法比冻结源改进41.8 MAE；在真实全分辨率语料上，自适应修复了密集场景少计问题，风险模块在2/6个片段中触发真实拥堵事件；消融证明归一化驱动而非流驱动，连续性残差对比例误差不变；移位门显示移位幅度与精度损伤秩无关。

Conclusion: 建议采用无条件自适应并配合尾部监控，提出六点部署协议，确保无人机计数在极端场景下可靠运行并及时预警危险人流。

Abstract: Saudi Arabia will host the 2034 FIFA World Cup and already operates crowd management at Hajj scale. Drone-based counting must hold accuracy on footage unlike anything in its training corpus, without labels, and must warn of dangerous inflow before a crush forms. We deliver a validated answer built on 525 controlled runs, a full-resolution corpus study, five falsification ablations, and a five-condition safety-interlock evaluation. Label-free adaptation recovers 31-49% of shift-induced error across four corruptions and five severities, with the strongest method gaining 41.8 MAE over the frozen source (95% CI [34.1, 49.6], p=7.5x10^-10, d=2.52). We establish a severity law separating methods with a constant absolute margin from the one whose margin grows, and a stability budget identifying which configuration is safe to fly. On a full-resolution corpus carrying a genuine +48 MAE aerial gap (source retrained to 14.6 validation MAE, a 34% improvement), adaptation repairs the dense-scene undercounting that would otherwise under-report a forming crush, and the flux-based risk module fires on real congestion episodes in 2 of 6 full-length clips. We localise the recoverable error: in a regime built to favor a physics-informed conservation prior (300-frame clips at 200ms spacing, five times wider than standard), the adaptation signal is normalisation-driven, not flow-driven; the continuity residual is invariant to the proportional counting errors domain shift produces, confirmed by four on/off ablations correlated at r=0.999 and a 40% input corruption moving accuracy by only 0.05 MAE. A label-free shift gate shows shift magnitude and accuracy damage are rank-independent (Spearman rho=0.20; rho=-0.60 among genuine shifts), quantifying the 58% of headroom a magnitude gate forgoes. We establish unconditional adaptation with tail monitoring as policy, closing with a six-point protocol.

</details>

*Satpreet Makhija*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了在确定性无环结构因果模型中，do-算子对应的图手术与机制替换在依赖层面等价，并刻画了模型图需精确记录依赖关系时该等价成立的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献常将do-算子描述为删除入边或替换机制，但两者分别在图与函数层面操作，缺乏严格的数学比较。本文旨在明确两者在因果依赖层面的等价关系，并澄清模型图包含多余箭头时的条件。

Method: 通过形式化定义机制族的依赖图Graph(F)，证明干预后的依赖图等于原依赖图的图手术结果；进一步分析当模型图G可能含多余箭头时等价成立的条件，并定义干预模型、刻画运行、组合序列干预及依赖祖先性。

Result: 主要定理表明Graph(F^ι)=Surg(Graph(F),T_ι)，即替换目标机制恰好删除了图手术所移除的依赖；对于含多余箭头的模型，等价性对所有干预成立当且仅当G精确记录F的依赖；此外，干预结果仅依赖实际依赖祖先。

Conclusion: 该研究为do-算子的两种表现提供了严格的数学基础，澄清了干预操作在依赖层面的本质，并给出了模型图精确性的条件，为因果推断提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: The $\operatorname{do}$-operator is described graphically by deleting arrows into its targets and functionally by replacing their mechanisms with constants. To call these operations equivalent is not yet a mathematical statement: one returns a graph and remembers only the targets, whereas the other returns mechanisms and also remembers the imposed values. We make a dependency-level comparison precise for deterministic acyclic structural causal models with finitely many endogenous variables. If $\operatorname{Graph}(F)$ extracts the dependencies of a mechanism family $F$, our main theorem is $\operatorname{Graph}(F^ι)=\operatorname{Surg}(\operatorname{Graph}(F),T_ι)$. Thus replacing target mechanisms removes exactly the dependencies removed by graph surgery. For a model $M=(G,F)$ whose graph may contain unused arrows, we characterize when the same equality holds with $G$ in place of $\operatorname{Graph}(F)$; it holds for every intervention exactly when $G$ records the dependencies of $F$ exactly. We then define the intervened model, characterize its run, show how sequential interventions combine, and prove that an outcome depends only on interventions at its actual dependency ancestors.

</details>

*Matthew T. Ford,Francis Bahk,Jingjing Wang,Adam S. Jovine,Tinghan Ye,David B. Shmoys,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大语言模型（LLM）在偏好判断中的自洽性问题，发现其基数偏好判断（如支付意愿）无法由单一效用函数一致地表示，存在显著且持久的不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖LLM生成的偏好判断来估计效用函数并据此决策，但未验证这些判断是否自洽。若判断不一致，基于效用函数的决策可能不可靠。

Method: 设计了统计检验和可解释的度量方法，衡量观察到的偏好响应与最佳拟合自洽效用函数之间的偏差，并在多个LLM和场景（航班、公寓、酒店）中进行实验。

Result: 实验显示所有测试的LLM均表现出较大且持久的不一致性，表明LLM偏好判断无法被单一效用函数忠实概括。

Conclusion: LLM生成的基数偏好判断缺乏自洽性，因此基于单一效用函数的决策方法需要重新评估或改进。

Abstract: Agents increasingly interpret a person's natural-language preferences by querying an LLM for numerical preference judgments, e.g., by asking how much the person would be willing to pay for an item. A growing body of work estimates a utility function from these judgments and then chooses actions based on their estimated utility. This pipeline assumes the judgments are approximately self-consistent: that a single utility function can reproduce them. But are they? To study this question, we measure the self-consistency of cardinal LLM preference judgments. For example, the difference in stated willingness-to-pay between two items should match the stated payment that makes a person indifferent to exchanging them. We develop statistical tests and interpretable measures of how far observed responses depart from the best-fitting self-consistent utility function. Experiments with flight, apartment, and hotel examples across six LLMs reveal large persistent inconsistencies. This suggests that LLM-derived preference judgments cannot be faithfully summarized by a single utility function.

</details>

*Kang Chen,Sihan Zhao,Yixin Cao,Yugang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于混合专家推理模型的两级内部读出方法，将词汇级语义空间压缩为64轴语义框架J64，并基于专家路由统计重构出低开销代理R64，以揭示模型推理过程的可读状态，支持测试时的决策制定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型生成的文本只是其内部推理过程的部分记录，传统方法仅通过外部输出（如token占用）难以捕捉模型的真实推理状态，如推理努力与问题压力的区分。

Method: 首先，将词汇规模的J空间蒸馏为64轴语义框架J64，该框架直接从模型自身的推理状态学习得到；其次，基于模型的原生专家路由统计重构J64，得到R64代理；最后，利用J64和R64进行测试时决策，包括候选集选择、加权投票，以及生成期间的累积停止和重采样策略。

Result: J64在推理状态可读性上优于基线（AUC提升0.096–0.135），R64与J64的中位轴相关系数达到0.69–0.86，并在gpt-oss-20b上保留95–100%的预测增益；在决策中，J64和R64改善单分支选择，R64加权投票在7/8设置中优于多数投票；生成期间，J64提升准确率1.1–5.9点，R64保留0.9–3.2点；路由器编辑验证了J64的机制解释。

Conclusion: J64使潜在过程状态可读，R64使路由可部署和可操作，两者结合为推理模型提供了有效的内部监控和干预手段。

Abstract: What a reasoning model writes is only a partial record of the process that produces it. We introduce a two-level internal readout for mixture-of-experts reasoning. We first distill vocabulary-scale J-space into J64, a 64-axis semantic frame learned from the model's own reasoning states. J64 reveals readable process state that the emitted trace does not show: it separates inference effort from problem-induced strain. It also adds 0.096 to 0.135 held-out AUC over a baseline that reads the same rollout as token occupancy and aggregates it in exactly the same way. We then reconstruct J64 from native expert-routing statistics. The result is R64, a low-overhead proxy: its median per-axis correlation with J64 is 0.69 to 0.86 across three models and two families, and on gpt-oss-20b it preserves 95 to 100% of J64's predictive gain. The readout supports test-time decisions at two temporal resolutions. Over completed candidate sets, J64 and R64 improve single-branch selection, and R64-weighted voting improves plain majority voting in seven of eight settings. During generation, rolling readout windows drive a cumulative stop-and-resample policy whose operating point is fixed on training questions alone. J64 improves accuracy by 1.1 to 5.9 points over a sibling-permuted control, and the routing-only R64 proxy retains 0.9 to 3.2 of those points. Finally, router edits aimed at the mechanism J64 names induce the predicted reasoning behaviors and shift a diagnosed stall from numerical guessing toward exact symbolic execution. Together, J64 makes latent process state readable, while routing makes it deployable and actionable.

</details>


### [45] [GraphWake: Group Polarization via Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade in LLM-Agent Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17665)
*Haoran Bu,Zejian Chen,Litian Zhang,Xi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为记忆介导的极化级联（Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade）的新型威胁，通过利用代理的记忆和公共讨论，仅通过暴露少量目标代理即可引发群体极化，并实例化为GraphWake框架，实验证明其能显著增加群体极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的操纵代理提示或构建回声室的方法在实践中难以实现，因此需要探索新的、更隐蔽的威胁模型来揭示代理社交平台可能面临的极化风险。

Method: 提出了记忆介导的极化级联威胁，包含三个阶段：暴露与记忆保留、检索与再现、迭代传播。实例化为GraphWake，包含三个组件：立场支持论证知识图谱、公理导向三元组选择和立场中性记忆提示。

Result: 在多个讨论和记忆系统上的实验表明，GraphWake显著增加了群体极化程度。

Conclusion: 揭示了社区层面的极化风险，提醒在代理社交平台设计中需考虑此类安全威胁。

Abstract: LLM-driven agents can autonomously exchange opinions on online platforms and form communities. Such agent-operated social platforms raise a new security concern: attackers may manipulate agents to induce group polarization. Existing methods manipulate agent prompts or construct echo chambers, both of which are difficult to realize in practice. We therefore formulate a new threat, Memory-Mediated Polarization Cascade, which uses agent memory as a persistence channel and public discussion as a propagation channel. This threat contains three stages. During exposure and memory retention, the attacker exposes a small set of target agents to arguments that reinforce their respective stated stances. The targets' memory systems then process and retain these arguments. During retrieval and reproduction, a shared stance-neutral discussion cues the targets to retrieve and reproduce their respective retained arguments. During iterative propagation, untreated agents influenced by the reproduced arguments restate and spread them. We instantiate this threat in GraphWake with three components: (i) stance-support argumentation knowledge graphs construct knowledge-based arguments; (ii) axiom-oriented triple selection distills them for reliable retention and reproduction; and (iii) stance-neutral memory cueing triggers concurrent retrieval and reproduction, initiating propagation. Experiments across multiple discussions and memory systems show that GraphWake substantially increases group polarization. These findings reveal a community-level polarization risk.

</details>


### [46] [Auditing Self-Evolution in Financial Agents: Capability Gains, Security Drift, and Execution-Interface Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17684)
*Jialong Li,Jialing Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对三种自进化智能体（SkillOpt、AWM、ReasoningBank）在模拟电子银行环境中进行审计，发现仅关注准确率会掩盖安全风险：虽然效用提升，但暴露度和攻击成功率可能上升，且需考虑工件与执行器的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体虽能提升性能，但现有评估仅关注进化后的准确率，未检验是否保留原有正确行为或存在安全隐患，需要更全面的审计方法。

Method: 在模拟电子银行环境中，使用匹配的良性获取轨迹、密封评估端点、执行接地检查和独立状态回放，对三种自进化智能体进行审计。

Result: SkillOpt提升效用但增加暴露与攻击成功率；ReasoningBank提升效用且不增加总体ASR；AWM因文本动作包络兼容性问题导致效用下降，移除后效用和风险均上升。

Conclusion: 审计自进化金融智能体需追踪回归、攻击面接触、未授权金融状态变化和工件执行器兼容性，而非仅依赖准确率。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents turn experience into reusable skills, workflows, or memories, but post-evolution accuracy alone does not show whether learned behavior preserves previously correct behavior or security. We audit SkillOpt, Agent Workflow Memory (AWM), and ReasoningBank in simulated e-banking using matched benign acquisition trajectories, sealed evaluation endpoints, execution-grounded checks, and independent state replay. On Qwen 3.7 Flash, SkillOpt raises benign utility from 0.741 to 0.837 while exposure to injected content rises from 0.820 to 0.943. Conditional attack success after exposure falls from 0.605 to 0.562, yet overall attack success rate (ASR) rises from 0.496 to 0.530 and unauthorized financial state changes rise to 0.685. Across three independently evolved lineages, capability, exposure, and unauthorized-state changes increase in all three, whereas ASR increases in only two. ReasoningBank raises utility to 0.859 without increasing aggregate ASR, although unauthorized state changes remain slightly above Static. AWM reveals a separate evaluation hazard: a literal WebArena text-action envelope disrupts tool execution in our native function-calling executor. In a post-hoc sensitivity test, removing only that envelope restores utility from 0.319 to 0.756, while exposure rises from 0.299 to 0.909 and ASR from 0.195 to 0.575. Auditing self-evolving financial agents therefore requires tracking regressions, attack-surface contact, unauthorized financial-state change, and artifact-executor compatibility, not accuracy alone.

</details>


### [47] [Mixture-of-Expert Blocks Contain Strong Hallucination Detection Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17687)
*Joao Fonseca,Rodrigo Rodrigues,Paolo Romano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InnerExpert，首个利用MoE架构内部信号（如路由器熵、专家分歧和使用模式）进行逐token幻觉检测的方法，在五个数据集和两种MoE架构上优于现有方法，仅需单次前向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法大多在答案或句子级别操作，无法定位幻觉片段。MoE架构提供了稠密模型中没有的内部信号，但尚未被利用，因此探索这些信号用于细粒度检测。

Method: InnerExpert结合路由级别信号（如路由器熵、专家分歧、专家使用模式）与标准transformer信号，形成紧凑的逐token特征向量，通过轻量分类器（用LLM-as-a-judge生成的标签训练）进行分类，支持持续更新。

Result: 在五个数据集和两种MoE架构上，InnerExpert优于现有方法，达到高达0.91的答案级AUROC和0.76的token级AUROC，且仅需单次前向传播。

Conclusion: MoE内部信号对于逐token幻觉检测有效，InnerExpert提供了无需人工标注的细粒度检测方案，具有实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Despite their widespread use, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain limited by a fundamental problem: the generation of plausible but false content, known as hallucinations. Most existing detection methods operate at the answer or sentence level, yet per-token detection is essential for localizing hallucinated spans and enabling fine-grained interventions. In this paper, we explore the use of the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) paradigm to address this gap. In MoE architectures, a single forward pass activates a sparse subset of experts (i.e., distinct feedforward networks per layer) via a routing mechanism, producing internal signals (e.g., router entropy, expert disagreement, and expert usage patterns) that are unavailable in dense architectures and have not been previously exploited for hallucination detection. To this end, we introduce InnerExpert, the first method to leverage these MoE-specific signals for per-token hallucination detection. InnerExpert combines routing-level and standard transformer signals into compact per-token feature vectors, classified by a lightweight detector trained on labels produced by an LLM-as-a-judge pipeline, which enables continuous model updates without manual annotation. Our results show that InnerExpert outperforms existing methods across five datasets and two MoE architectures, achieving up to 0.91 answer-level and 0.76 token-level AUROC, while requiring only a single forward pass.

</details>


### [48] [On the Fragility of Self-Improving Agents: Variance, Task Order, and Underspecification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18066)
*Qinyuan Ye,Yu Li,Yada Pruksachatkun,Jiaxin Zhang,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了基于记忆的自我改进智能体，发现其性能受任务顺序和评估噪声影响显著，且任务/环境规格不足是脆弱性的原因之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆型自我改进智能体方法在可靠性方面未被充分研究，评估通常忽略方差和任务顺序影响。

Method: 对两种方法进行多次运行和任务随机排序的实验，并手动检查记忆内容，通过添加详细规则和环境反馈验证假设。

Result: 发现评估噪声大、任务顺序依赖强；添加规格信息部分缓解性能下降，但仍存在差距。

Conclusion: 呼吁更严格的评估协议（多次运行、压力测试），并强调人类监督系统的重要性以防止不可预见的失败。

Abstract: Memory-based self-improving agents--those that learn from an online stream of tasks and improve over time by maintaining a textual memory bank--have shown great promise in recent literature. However, the reliability aspects of these methods have been critically overlooked. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive re-evaluation of two memory-based methods, broadening the scope of evaluation along two axes: (1) including multiple runs to quantify variance, and (2) randomly shuffling the tasks to investigate the effect of task order. Through these experiments, we make two observations that expose the fragility of current methods: First, agent evaluation is inherently noisy in complex environments and on multi-step tasks, and stacking a self-improving loop on top can further amplify this noise. Second, the agent's improvement is highly dependent on task order. Prior works often adopt default orderings that impose an implicit curriculum, acting as a hidden prerequisite for success.
  To better understand this fragility, we manually examine the agents' memory and hypothesize that task and environment underspecification contribute to this fragility. We validate this hypothesis by incorporating information that enables better specification, such as detailed rubrics and environment feedback, into the memory construction process. While this added information partially closes the performance degradation in previous experiments, significant gaps still remain, suggesting that other uncharacterized factors contribute to this fragility. Looking ahead, our work advocates for more rigorous evaluation protocols for self-improving agents by reporting results across multiple runs and stress-testing them under challenging conditions. Moreover, our findings on underspecification call for systems and interfaces that enable effective human oversight, preventing agents from failing in unforeseeable ways.

</details>


### [49] [Accuracy and Robustness of Model Cascades Under Data Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17711)
*Pallavi Mitra,Jai Kushwaha,Felix Biessmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于置信度的模型级联在图像分类中面对输入退化时的路由可靠性，识别出三种失效模式，并强调需在分布偏移下评估路由质量而不仅是干净准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型级联通过将简单样本路由到小模型、困难样本延迟到大模型来降低AI能耗，但其有效性依赖于置信度路由的可靠性；输入退化可能改变置信度和路由决策，现有评估多关注干净数据下的准确率，忽视了这一风险。

Method: 在帕累托最优的模型级联（平衡准确率、路由质量和能耗）上，系统性地施加静态腐蚀和序列扰动，分析路由决策的变化，并揭示失效模式。

Result: 发现三种失效模式：静态腐蚀要么破坏路由信号（大模型仍有用），要么使两模型退化（延迟无法恢复准确率）；序列扰动则导致预测稳定但延迟被抑制，产生稳定但不可靠的预测。级联在干净数据上可达10倍CO2减排。

Conclusion: 能源高效的模型级联需要超越干净准确率的评估，必须明确关注分布偏移下的路由可靠性。

Abstract: Prediction cascades significantly reduce energy consumption of Artificial Intelligence (AI) models while maintaining high predictive performance. The idea is that easy inputs are routed through a lightweight small model, and difficult uncertain cases are deferred to a larger model. While this design can improve computational efficiency on clean data, its effectiveness depends on the reliability of confidence-based routing. Input degradations, such as static corruptions and sequential perturbations, can shift model confidence and routing decisions. In this paper, we study confidence-based cascade frameworks for image classification and investigate how such degradations affect their confidence-based deferral behavior. We select a model cascade at the pareto-optimum of accuracy, routing quality, and energy consumption that achieves competitive predictive performance with an up to 10-fold decrease in CO$_2$ emissions. We study the behavior of that model cascade under input corruptions and analyze how the cascade's routing decisions change when the input distribution shifts. Our analysis identifies three failure modes. Static corruptions either (1) break the routing signal while the large model remains useful, or (2) degrade both models so deferral no longer recovers accuracy. Sequential perturbations reveal a third mode: predictions stabilize but deferral suppresses, yielding stable but unreliable predictions. These findings demonstrate that energy efficient model cascades require evaluation beyond clean accuracy, with explicit attention to routing reliability under distribution shift.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Suspicious Steps: Ontological Trust in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17718)
*An He,Yao Wang,Haibin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出本体论信任（ontological trust）概念，并实现为RGE在线监控器，用于检测长时程智能体轨迹前缀中的漂移。RGE通过分解角色、目标和证据进行确定性状态更新，在多个基准上优于现有基线，且在漂移检测和良性覆盖间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体在执行多步骤任务时，轨迹前缀可能逐渐偏离用户授权的任务，而现有监控方法多关注局部合规性或最终结果，难以捕捉这种前缀级别的语义漂移。

Method: RGE（Role-Goal-Evidence）在线监控器，使用LLM仅提取结构化任务和步骤表示，而信任状态更新、投影和干预决策均为确定性计算，输出可重放、可审计的信任轨迹。

Result: 在OSWorld、FinanceBench和EICU-AC数据集上，RGE在漂移检测（尤其是前缀配对漂移）上优于规则、评委和盾牌基线；使用两个较大估计模型时，所有基准的漂移F1超过93%，良性覆盖率不低于95.8%。伪一致性检测更具挑战性，其性能受任务完成是否外部可见的结构性限制。

Conclusion: RGE能有效在线检测长时程任务中的前缀级漂移，同时保持高良性覆盖率；伪一致性限制表明需进一步研究任务可见性对监控的影响。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly operate across many steps, tools, and observa- tions. In this setting, the relevant oversight question is not only whether each action is locally valid, but whether the evolving trajectory still corresponds to the task the user authorized. Drift can accumulate quietly: an agent may call the right tool with plausible arguments at every step, while its prefix moves toward a broader role, an adjacent objective, or evidence the user never supplied. Existing monitors mostly check local compliance, deliver final-trace verdicts, or score generic risk; they do not directly estimate this prefix-level relation. We introduce ontological trust, a task-conditioned property of trajectory prefixes, and instantiate it as RGE, an online monitor that decomposes trust along Role, Goal, and Evidence. RGE uses LLMs only to derive structured task and step representations; trust-state updates, projec- tions, and intervention decisions are deterministic, so the output is a replayable and auditable trust trajectory rather than a single end-to-end judge verdict. We construct a cross-domain trajectory corpus from OSWorld, FinanceBench, and EICU-AC, covering benign executions, prefix-paired drift, and pseudo-consistency failures. On this corpus, RGE outperforms adapted rule-, judge-, and shield-style baselines on prefix-paired drift detection. With the two larger estimator models, it exceeds 93% Drift F1 on every benchmark while keeping benign coverage at or above 95.8%. Pseudo-consistency is harder: detection depends on whether task completion is externally visible, a structural limit we characterize empirically.

</details>


### [51] [The Curious Case of Exploding DecPOMDPs: Containing the Fire through Policy Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17749)
*Nazlı Nur Karabulut,tanya Braun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种策略计数的方法来解决DecPOMDP中智能体数量带来的指数复杂性，并开发了相应的动态规划算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DecPOMDP在智能体数量上存在指数复杂性，已有的对称性分区方法虽能压缩模型，但策略空间仍然爆炸，因此需要新方法。

Method: 将计数从智能体转移到策略上，定义策略计数DecPOMDP，并设计基于紧凑表示的策略计数动态规划算法。

Result: 算法在策略计数DecPOMDP上实现了高效求解，使得智能体数量上的计算复杂性得到缓解。

Conclusion: 策略计数是处理多智能体决策问题可扩展性的有效方向。

Abstract: Decentralised partially observable Markov decision processes (DecPOMDPs) provide a general framework for modelling multi-agent decision making under uncertainty. However, DecPOMDPs are known to suffer from exponential complexity in the number of agents. One way to combat this intractability in agent numbers is to look at partitions of agents that exhibit a form of symmetry among agents, allowing for a compact encoding by counting. However, a challenge arises as the policy space explodes, even though the model complexity and evaluation cost reduce to a polynomial dependence. In this paper, we redirect our focus from counting agents to counting policies, which actually enables tractability in agent numbers for so called policy-counted DecPOMDPs. Further, we present policy-counted dynamic programming using the compact representation to solve policy-counted DecPOMDPs efficiently.

</details>


### [52] [Evaluating the Diversity of AI-Generated Content with Diversity Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17731)
*Xiuyuan Hu,Xuege Hou,Guoqing Liu,Yang Zhao,Jieran Li,Dongbiao Sun,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Hao Zhang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出将生成内容多样性简化为单个标量指标存在本质上的不确定性，并提出多样性轮廓（diversity profiles）作为曲线值、条件感知的评估框架，以提供更透明和分辨率感知的多样性比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多样性评估方法将样本在嵌入空间中的距离聚合为单一标量分数，但不同指标可能对同一组样本产生矛盾排序，且高维空间中距离分布集中且依赖模态，导致评估结果不稳定且缺乏共识。

Method: 结合公理化分析（证明常见标量指标无法同时满足所有理想性质）和实证分析（展示高维表示空间导致距离分布问题），提出多样性轮廓：在指定表示和距离/核函数下，对参数化多样性族在阈值、尺度、指数或阶次范围内进行计算，形成曲线而非单点，用于判断比较的稳健性。

Result: 为多种代表性多样性度量族实例化轮廓，并在生成式AI评估中展示其实际应用，证明轮廓能揭示比较是否在不同分辨率下保持稳健，避免任意参数选择带来的误导。

Conclusion: 多样性轮廓提供了更透明、分辨率感知的评估框架，有助于更可靠和可解释地比较AI生成内容的多样性。

Abstract: Diversity is a fundamental criterion for evaluating generative artificial intelligence (AI) systems, yet its measurement remains inherently ambiguous. Existing approaches typically represent generated samples in an embedding space, compute pairwise distances or similarities, and aggregate them into a single scalar score. Such scalar summaries are convenient, but they often encode different inductive biases and may yield contradictory rankings of the same sample sets. In this paper, we argue that diversity evaluation for AI-generated content is intrinsically under-specified when reduced to a single number. We first review representative diversity metrics, and then diagnose their limitations from two complementary perspectives: an axiomatic analysis showing that no representative scalar metric satisfies all desirable properties simultaneously, and an empirical analysis showing that high-dimensional representation spaces can induce concentrated, modality-dependent distance distributions. To address these issues, we propose diversity profiles: curve-valued, condition-aware summaries that evaluate a parameterized diversity family across a range of thresholds, scales, exponents, or orders under a specified representation and distance or kernel function. Diversity profiles reveal whether a comparison is robust across resolutions or instead depends on an arbitrary parameter choice. We instantiate profiles for several representative metric families and demonstrate their practical use in generative AI evaluation. Overall, diversity profiles provide a more transparent and resolution-aware framework for comparing the diversity of AI-generated content.

</details>


### [53] [D$^2$ACCI: A Dual-Loop Diagnostic Protocol for Evidence-Preserving Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17756)
*Xule Liu,Yijun Liu,Chao Li,Shao Kun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 D²ACCI，一种双循环协议，通过诊断门控、配对统计检验和受保护切片监控，实现记忆系统迭代的局部化失败诊断；在三个基准上取得最优结果，并证明统计显著改进与特征标志保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端评估只能显示错误发生，无法定位是记忆管线的哪一阶段（摄取、检索、过滤或生成）导致的失败；且缺乏配对比较、非回归检查和阶段级诊断跟踪。

Method: 设计了双循环协议 D²ACCI，包含外部的诊断门（促进或拒绝干预）和内部的迭代控制；引入 DCR 指标衡量可观测性分级，并开发 D²ACCI-Eval 用于门控重放；实例化为 MemStack 系统。

Result: 在 LoCoMo、LongMemEval、PersonaMem-V2 上分别达到 93.59%、90.93%、57.20%；五项配对消融显示补充提取、会话记忆检索和遗忘保护带来 1.9-3.7pp 的显著提升；诊断工件达到 98-100% DCR@3，而仅结果日志为 0%。

Conclusion: 可靠的记忆系统迭代需要可追踪、统计基础和回归感知的证据，D²ACCI 填补了这一空白，并为提升失败可定位性和证据质量提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Memory is a key capability of LLM agents. Persistent memory extends this across sessions---enabling recall, revision, and personalization. Yet its multi-stage pipeline (ingestion, retrieval, filtering, generation) makes failures difficult to localize: end-to-end evaluation reveals that an error occurred, but not which stage caused it. Existing evaluations often report aggregate performance without paired statistical comparisons, slice-level non-regression checks, or stage-level diagnostic traces. We propose D$^2$ACCI (Diagnostic-Driven Artifact-based Closed-loop Controlled Iteration), a dual-loop protocol whose outer diagnostic gate promotes, feature-flags, or rejects memory interventions based on paired evidence, protected-slice monitoring, and trace-level localizability. We further introduce DCR, a graded observability metric that measures whether failures remain localizable, and D$^2$ACCI-Eval, a reusable artifact for gate replay. We instantiate the protocol in MemStack and evaluate on three public benchmarks, achieving 93.59% on LoCoMo, 90.93% on LongMemEval, and 57.20% on PersonaMem-V2. Five paired ablations show that supplement extraction, session-memory retrieval, and Forget Guard yield statistically significant gains (+1.9 to +3.7pp, all p $\le$ .003). In contrast, BM25/RRF is retained as a monitored feature flag---a distinction invisible to aggregate-only evaluation. A diagnostic audit shows enriched traces substantially improve root-cause agreement over result-only relabeling. Diagnostic artifacts reach 98--100% DCR@3 versus 0% for results-only logs. These results establish that robust memory-system iteration demands traceable, statistically grounded, and regression-aware evidence---exactly the gap D$^2$ACCI fills.

</details>


### [54] [Neuro-symbolic learning over OWL 2 DL via consequence-based compilation to differentiable circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17741)
*Olga Mashkova,Asaad Mohammedsaleh,Fernando Zhapa-Camacho,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 解析错误


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析错误

Method: 解析错误

Result: 解析错误

Conclusion: 解析错误

Abstract: OWL 2 DL ontologies, grounded in the description logic $\mathcal{SROIQ}$, express large knowledge bases in biomedicine and the Semantic Web. Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) learners over description logics either embed the ontology in a continuous space, abandoning classical entailment, or restrict to the Horn fragment $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$, which has a single canonical model. We present Baobab, which compiles a $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ ontology with a finite ABox into a Sentential Decision Diagram (SDD): it saturates a propositional core under a consequence-based calculus and instantiates the remaining $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ features (nominals, number restrictions, and the role axioms) over the active domain. The SDD's evidence-conditioned weighted model count then trains a perception network to recognize real images under partial ABox supervision: on an ontology that exercises every distinctive $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ feature, a CNN learns to read MNIST digits coupled by a successor relation and recovers latent ontology concepts that an independent perception leaves at chance. When the supervision admits several ontology-consistent completions, an independent perception collapses onto one, a reasoning shortcut: we show that a mixture indexed by the query's justifications can represent the calibrated posterior no independent perception can, and that seeding it from the circuit's enumerated completions attains the Bayes-optimal posterior on a real-image MNIST task where single-WMC and learned mixtures (the BEARS-ensemble hypothesis class) do not: to our knowledge the first to characterize and mitigate reasoning shortcuts in a non-Horn description logic. Soundness of the compiler and the representation result are machine-checked in Lean 4. Code is available at https://github.com/bio-ontology-research-group/baobab.

</details>


### [55] [StartupBench: Benchmarking General-Purpose Agents on Market-Validated End-to-End Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17800)
*Liya Zhu,Xin Ma,Tao Liu,Haodong Wang,Ge Zhang,Jingzhe Ding,Qingshui Gu,Yongjie Zhong,Jinxiang Meng,Yuan Gao,Yunqiu Zhou,Hao Zhu,Jifeng He,Yongzhi Liao,Xinyi Zhang,Chaoxin Li,Yi Zhu,Xi Lin,Duju Zeng,Xiang Gao,Wen Zhang,Yunyang Wang,Duo Wang,Huan Zhou,Zuo Wang,Jin Chen,Kaiyuan Zhang,Chuqian Yu,Tianhao Yu,Longxiang Liu,Jianbo Xue,Huimin Che,Jiahao Wang,Yujia Qin,Jiaheng Liu,Shen Yan,Xiaolong Chang,Wenhao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StartupBench，一个基于市场验证的AI创业产品的端到端代理基准，发现现有最强模型仅能完成约30%的任务，揭示当前通用代理在真实世界任务上的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多依赖研究者选定的任务，无法反映真实用户对AI系统的实际需求，因此需要从市场验证的AI产品中提取真实工作流来评估代理能力。

Method: 系统研究具有实际采用率的AI产品及其工作流和用户，将工作流转化为完整的、面向交付物的任务，并用细粒度评分标准评估；在统一代理框架下测试多个代表性模型。

Result: 最强模型在StartupBench上仅约30%任务成功完成，但许多任务有部分进展；复杂指令遵循和领域专业知识是主要失败来源。

Conclusion: 许多市场验证的工作流超出当前通用代理的可靠能力，StartupBench可作为衡量真实世界任务端到端完成进度的实证基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models(LLMs) and agents have substantially improved the ability of AI systems to execute complex tasks. Yet existing benchmarks largely rely on researcher-selected tasks, leaving uncertain whether such progress extends to the work that real-world users actually demand from AI systems. We introduce \textbf{StartupBench}, an E2E agent benchmark grounded in market-validated AI startup products. Rather than defining tasks from pre-defined assumptions about useful agent capabilities, we systematically study AI products with demonstrated adoption, together with their product workflows and users, to identify real-world tasks for which AI has established practical demand across diverse professional domains. We translate these workflows into complete deliverable-oriented tasks and evaluate them with fine-grained rubrics capturing their complex requirements. Across representative models evaluated under a unified agent harness, even the strongest model successfully completes only approximately 30\% of StartupBench, despite making substantial partial progress on many tasks. Further analysis identifies aspects like complex instruction following and domain-specific expertise as major sources of failure. Our results reveal that many market-validated workflows remain beyond the reliable capabilities of current general-purpose agents, establishing StartupBench as an empirical measure of progress toward E2E completions of real-world user tasks.

</details>


### [56] [ARASH: Adaptive Retrieval And Shot Selection for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17856)
*Samirasadat Jamalidinan,Yue Xu,Kazem Cheshmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARASH方法，通过局部邻域分析选择最优样本作为上下文示例，显著减少TabPFN的提示长度和内存使用，同时保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格预测中，大型语言模型和表格基础模型（TFM）计算成本高，频繁重训练不现实；少样本提示虽高效，但难以选择最相关的行作为示例。

Method: ARASH：基于训练集局部邻域分析的自适应、查询特定的检索与样本选择方法，为每个查询选出最佳上下文示例。

Result: 在TabPFN上，ARASH将提示长度减少1261.5倍，内存使用减少2.56倍，同时精度与原始方法相当。

Conclusion: ARASH能有效提升TFM的效率，为表格数据中的上下文学习提供实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular prediction is a critical task across numerous applications. The recent success of large language models has sparked various approaches for adapting them to the tabular domain. A prevalent strategy involves training or fine-tuning specialized Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) such as TabPFN. However, TFMs require substantial computational resources, and frequent model retraining is often impractical. In-context learning (ICL), specifically, few-shot prompting, offers a resource-efficient alternative to enhance performance. Yet, identifying the most relevant rows to serve as shots remains a challenge for tabular data. This paper introduces ARASH (Adaptive, query-specific Retrieval And Shot selection), a method that improves TFM efficiency by selecting optimal shots based on local neighborhood analysis within the training set. Our results demonstrate that ARASH reduces the prompt length and memory usage of TabPFN by 1261.5$\times$ and 2.56$\times$, respectively, while providing comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [57] [AutoResearch: Insight In, Hallucination Out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17906)
*Yiming Ren,Xiang Liu,Qumeng Sun,Xiao Zhang,Jiahao Li,Haoyang Zhang,Junjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoResearch，一个两阶段自主研究系统，通过想法生成与执行分离，确保研究过程科学严谨，实验结论可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究系统能执行长流程，但自动化不保证科学性和可靠性，需要确保想法形成和实验结论都经过严格验证。

Method: 两阶段系统：Idea Generation阶段集成研究信号与领域知识，识别可迁移机制，用多模型生成和交叉评审产生可测试计划；Idea Execution阶段用协调代理分解实验、迭代实施诊断，并采用独立基于证据的评审。

Result: 在跨模态检索、系统优化和基准驱动ML中，AutoResearch产生可测量进步，检测纠正不可靠结果，如RSICD上平均Recall从32.84提升到34.69，审计确认问题事件仅5次，远低于其他系统（11-27次）。

Conclusion: AutoResearch实现了“洞察输入，幻觉输出”的研究流程，即实验前确保想法有依据，接受前确保结论有证据，提高了自主研究的可靠性和有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous research systems are increasingly capable of executing long research workflows, yet automation alone does not ensure that the resulting process remains scientifically grounded. We introduce AutoResearch, a two-stage system that connects Idea Generation with Idea Execution to address both how research ideas are formed and how they are reliably established through experimentation. In Idea Generation, AutoResearch continuously integrates emerging research signals with accumulated domain knowledge, identifies transferable mechanistic insights, and uses multi-model generation and cross-review to produce grounded, testable research plans. In Idea Execution, coordinated agents decompose these plans into experiments, iteratively implement and diagnose them, and employ independent evidence-based review before accepting research conclusions. Across representative settings in cross-modal retrieval, systems optimization, and benchmark-driven machine learning, AutoResearch turns generated ideas into measurable progress, detects and corrects unreliable experimental results, and makes evidence-conditioned decisions to continue, revise, or terminate research directions. For example, on RSICD benchmark, an AutoResearch-generated idea improves mean Recall from 32.84 to 34.69, while recording only 5 audit-confirmed issue events compared with 11-27 for other autonomous research systems. These results demonstrate a research process in which meaningful insight is grounded before experimentation and conclusions are grounded before acceptance: Insight In, Hallucination Out.

</details>


### [58] [EvoTS-Agent: A Self-Evolving LLM Agent for Financial Time Series Change Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17933)
*Lei Jiang,Ye Wei,Xinyu Xi,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Yifan Bao,Raad Khraishi,Yihao Ang,Anthony K. H. Tung,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LLM的自演化智能体EvoTS-Agent，用于金融时间序列变点检测，通过验证反馈引导的轨迹演化，适应不同数据集特性，超越现有LLM方法并保持100%执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列具有非平稳性和异质性，单一无监督算法无法在不同资产和市场状态下表现一致，传统工作流依赖专家经验，缺乏可扩展性和适应性。

Method: EvoTS-Agent首先进行探索性数据分析（EDA）以表征数据并初始化候选模型，然后通过三个算子演化实验轨迹：Revision（利用当前最佳解）、Alternative Strategy（停滞时探索不同建模方向）、Recombination（综合高绩效轨迹的互补证据），并以验证反馈指导演化。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，EvoTS-Agent一致优于现有基于LLM的智能体，并在所有评估的骨干LLM上保持100%的执行成功率。

Conclusion: 验证反馈引导的轨迹演化能有效适应不同数据集的统计特性，实现可靠的自动变点检测，减少对专家干预的依赖。

Abstract: Financial time series exhibit non-stationary and heterogeneous statistical properties, making change-point detection challenging because no single unsupervised algorithm performs consistently across assets and market regimes. Conventional workflows consequently depend heavily on expert-driven model selection, feature design, and hyperparameter tuning, limiting their scalability and adaptability. We propose EvoTS-Agent, a validation-guided self-evolving LLM agent for autonomous financial time-series change-point detection. EvoTS-Agent first performs curated exploratory data analysis to characterize dataset properties and initialize candidate detection models. It then evolves executable experiment trajectories through three complementary operators: \textit{Revision} exploits the current best solution, \textit{Alternative Strategy} explores fundamentally different modeling directions when progress stagnates, and \textit{Recombination} synthesizes complementary evidence from high-performing trajectories. Validation feedback guides trajectory evolution throughout the search, enabling the agent to adapt its detection pipeline to the statistical characteristics of each dataset while preserving reliable optimization. Experiments across four benchmark datasets demonstrate that EvoTS-Agent consistently outperforms existing LLM-based agents while maintaining a 100\% execution success rate across all evaluated backbone LLMs.

</details>


### [59] [Adaptive Policy Portfolios for Robust Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17929)
*Kasper Engelen,Sebastian Junges,Guillermo A. Pérez,Marnix Suilen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了自适应策略组合（adaptive policy portfolios）在鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）中的认证与合成问题，证明了其计算复杂性，并提出了一种可离线构建且支持运行时特化的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RMDP通过优化单个策略以应对一组转移函数，但当真实动态固定且部分可识别时，这种方法可能过于保守。自适应策略组合通过离线合成多个策略并在线轻量选择，能更好地适应环境，而相关遗憾目标的复杂性尚不明确。

Method: 本文采用计算复杂性理论分析，定义了鲁棒遗憾（robust regret）并研究认证与合成问题的复杂性，同时提出一种离线策略组合构建方法。

Result: 证明了对给定组合进行认证在确定性组合和acyclic RMDP中为∀ℝ-完全，合成有界大小组合在一般有理多面体下为∃∀ℝ-完全，单策略情形也困难。最后给出了可离线构建并支持运行时特化的方法。

Conclusion: 自适应策略组合的认证与合成问题在计算上具有高度复杂性，但离线构建与在线选择结合仍是可行的方向，且所提构建方法具有实用性。

Abstract: Robust Markov decision processes optimize one policy against a set of plausible transition functions. This can be conservative when the unknown dynamics are fixed and become partially identifiable after deployment. We study adaptive policy portfolios: finite sets of memoryless randomized policies synthesized offline and paired with a lightweight online selector. Robust regret is a natural measure of portfolio quality: for each plausible environment, it measures the loss of the best portfolio member relative to the policy that would have been optimal had that environment been known. Related regret objectives were studied by Ghavamzadeh et al. (2016) with an emphasis on approximations and relaxations for safe policy improvement. We give a complexity-theoretic account of portfolio certification and synthesis. Certifying a given portfolio is $\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete already for deterministic portfolios in acyclic (s,a)-rectangular RMDPs. Synthesizing a portfolio of unary-bounded size is $\exists\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete for general rational polytopes, even with fixed discount and acyclic dynamics. The single-policy case is already hard, both combinatorially and algebraically. Finally, we present an offline portfolio construction that is amenable to runtime specialization.

</details>


### [60] [Towards Zero-Shot Task Transfer with Neurosymbolic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17959)
*Isidoro Tamassia,Lennert De Smet,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号世界模型，将奖励预测与观测重建解耦，使得模型能够零样本适应新的奖励函数，并展示了其优于纯神经方法的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的强化学习方法学习神经世界模型，虽然灵活但通常依赖任务，潜在表示不可解释且难以泛化到新任务。

Method: 通过仅依赖潜在状态中结构化符号组件的子集进行奖励预测，同时将观测重建与奖励预测解耦，学习神经符号世界模型。

Result: 该方法能够在无需额外环境交互的情况下，对新奖励函数实现零样本适应，并在泛化性能上优于纯神经方法。

Conclusion: 神经符号世界模型具有更好的泛化能力，但面临一些挑战，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based reinforcement learning methods learn neural world models that allow policy improvement by planning in a latent space, without assumptions on the structure of the underlying environment. While expressive, these models are generally task-dependent: they learn uninterpretable latent representations that are tied to the training task and thus hard to generalize to new tasks. In this work, we present a novel world model formulation where the reward prediction only depends on a subset of structured, symbolic components of the whole latent state. Decoupling observation reconstruction and reward prediction allows us to learn world models that can adapt zero-shot, i.e. without further environment interactions, to new reward functions defined over the same symbolic state space. We discuss the main advantages and challenges of learning these neurosymbolic world models and demonstrate the strong generalisation properties of our approach over purely neural methods.

</details>


### [61] [Procedural Content Metageneration via Program Search and Continual Abstraction Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17947)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过语言模型突变和交叉进化完整Python程序来生成程序化内容（如Sokoban等游戏关卡），并提出持续抽象发现（CAD）方法从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语到辅助模块，实验表明CAD在所有领域和API组合中提升了最终最佳适应度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能生成可执行程序，但现有方法多直接搜索个体关卡，而非程序生成器。研究者希望探索直接进化程序生成器，并通过提取可复用抽象来提升搜索效率。

Method: 使用语言模型进行突变和交叉操作，进化完整的Python生成器。引入持续抽象发现（CAD），从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语到运行专属的辅助模块。进行2x2实验，交叉CAD与固定手写领域API，共160次完整运行，每单元至少10次50代运行。

Result: CAD在所有八个领域与API对比中提高了平均最终最佳适应度。在CAD运行中，学习到的库被大多数后续程序采用，并反复发现验证、可达性和结构性实用工具。

Conclusion: 发现可复用原语能改进进化程序搜索用于内容生成，支持CAD方法在程序生成器进化中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models can generate executable programs, which makes it possible to search directly over procedural content generators rather than individual levels. We study this approach in Sokoban, Zelda, Dangerous Dave, and Lode Runner. Each run evolves complete Python generators through language-model mutation and crossover. We introduce Continual Abstraction Discovery, or CAD, which extracts reusable primitives from high-fitness programs into a run-specific helper module. A 2x2 experiment crosses CAD with access to a fixed hand-written domain API. The completed data set contains 160 complete runs, with at least ten 50-generation runs in every cell. CAD raises mean final best fitness in all eight domain and API comparisons. Across all CAD runs, learned libraries are adopted by most later programs and repeatedly rediscover validation, reachability, and structural utilities. These results support that discovering reusable primitives improves evolutionary program search for content generators.

</details>


### [62] [Can Large Language Models Explain Flight Safety Events? A Prior-Guided Semantic LLM-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18017)
*Lu Xu,Xu Li,Linjiang Zheng,Fan Li,Riquan Zhang,Jiaxing Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型的先验引导语义解释方法FlightLLM，用于可解释的飞行安全分析，在硬着陆数据集上实现了有竞争力的分类性能和直接合理的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI技术（如特征重要性图）需要大量领域知识才能转化为操作性解释，而LLM在语言推理方面有优势，但直接应用面临模态不一致、分类能力有限、数据稀缺和缺乏领域知识等挑战。

Method: 先进行特征工程（统计描述符+物理指标）解决模态不一致；然后用语义离散化模块将数值模式转换为定性描述；引入CatBoost作为统计专家，将其预测结果注入提示作为先验引导；采用对比少样本学习补偿数据不足；设计结构化提示嵌入航空领域知识。

Result: 在704个真实A320飞行样本（硬着陆事件）上，FlightLLM实现了有竞争力的分类性能，并生成了直接合理的因果解释。

Conclusion: FlightLLM有效结合LLM与统计模型，克服了应用挑战，为飞行安全分析提供了可解释且性能良好的解决方案。

Abstract: Improving flight safety with flight data requires not only accurate detection of risk events, but more importantly, clear interpretation of their underlying causes at the level of pilot control behavior. Existing explainable AI techniques, such as feature importance maps, often require considerable domain knowledge to translate them into operationally meaningful explanations. Large Language Models (LLMs), which excel at language reasoning, bring a promising solution to this issue. However, applying LLMs in this domain presents key challenges such as modal inconsistency, limited classification ability, scarcity of task-specific data for fine-tuning, and lack of domain knowledge. To overcome these challenges, we propose FlightLLM, a prior-guided semantic LLM-based approach for interpretable flight safety analysis. Specifically, we first perform feature engineering to address modal inconsistency, combining statistical descriptors with physically meaningful flight indicators. This representation is further processed by a Semantic Discretization module, which converts abstract numerical patterns into qualitative descriptions that are more compatible with language reasoning. In addition, since LLMs are not inherently strong classifiers, CatBoost is incorporated as a statistical expert, and its prediction results are injected into the prompt as prior guidance. A contrastive few-shot learning strategy is further adopted to compensate for limited data. Finally, we design structured prompts to embed aviation-specific knowledge into the inference process. Using hard landing, a representative risk event with complex causal mechanisms, as an anchor point, we evaluate FlightLLM on a dataset of 704 real-world A320 flight samples. Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves competitive classification performance while generating direct and reasonable explanations for event causes.

</details>


### [63] [HLSR: Hybrid Live Forecast Selective Dynamic Vehicle Rerouting for Real-Time Congestion Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18056)
*Xiao Wang,Shun Ren Yang,Hui Nien Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HLSR框架，通过融合实时与预测速度、双阈值拥堵检测和有限干预范围的路径重规划，在缓解城市交通拥堵的同时降低对车辆的干预需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络级实时最短路径重规划在模拟中高效，但实际中无法让所有车辆每周期都重规划，且纯实时或纯预测方法各有局限。

Method: 构建HLSR框架，包含双阈值拥堵检测、上游车辆选择、驾驶员个性化行程时间预测、接近车辆扩展、行程时间加权k最短路径生成，以及基于时间层级的混合实时-预测速度用于多成本路线分配。

Result: 在有限干预比例下，HLSR显著降低网络总行驶时间，并减少不必要的路线变更，有效缓解拥堵。

Conclusion: HLSR通过选择性融合实时与预测信息，在现实干预条件下实现了接近全干预方案的效益，为实际部署提供了可行的重路由策略。

Abstract: Urban traffic congestion reduces productivity and increases travel cost and emissions. Network-wide live travel-time shortest-path rerouting can be highly effective in simulation, but assumes that essentially every on-road vehicle is replanned every decision period. We propose HLSR, a selective hybrid live--forecast vehicle rerouting framework that fuses live edge speeds with short-horizon forecasts under limited intervention scope. Building on dual-threshold congestion detection, calibrated upstream selection, and driver-tailored travel-time prediction, HLSR further introduces approaching-vehicle expansion, travel-time-weighted k-shortest-path generation, and a horizon-dependent hybrid live--forecast segment speed used in multi-cost route allocation.

</details>


### [64] [StagedWorkspace: A Versioned Workspace for Knowledge-Work Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18050)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Yifan Zhou,Akshay Kalose,Cyrus Ayubcha,Levi Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StagedWorkspace，一个面向知识工作智能体的版本化工作区，通过将解析视图和审查差异绑定到原生文件的内容哈希，解决不同版本间的一致性问题。实验表明，双解析/原生访问显著提升性能，并强调了工作区状态作为实验变量的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识工作智能体在处理持续变化的数字工件时，其搜索、编辑、审查和提交的视图可能对应不同版本，导致状态不一致。编码智能体已有仓库契约，但PDF、电子表格、幻灯片等格式缺乏类似机制，影响任务完成质量。

Method: 提出StagedWorkspace，绑定解析记录和审查差异到原生文件的内容哈希，实现版本化工作区。通过固定消融实验和配对审查轴消融，比较不同视图（解析、原生、双访问）在OfficeQA Pro和APEX-Agents上的效果，并评估差异可见性的影响。

Result: 在固定消融中，双访问对所有模型均获得最高性能，OfficeQA Pass@1提升8.3-12.1点，APEX平均评分提升4.7-9.2点。SW-AGENT在OfficeQA上得63.9%（Gemini 3.1 Pro），APEX上得42.1（GPT-5.4 Nano），优于发表的同模型分数。审查轴消融显示差异可见时得分更高。

Conclusion: 工作区状态是知识工作智能体的关键实验变量，证据表明版本化工作区能显著提升性能。未来基准应显式评估证据、暂存编辑和提交工件作为状态转换。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly perform knowledge work (i.e., produce and modify persistent digital artifacts such as code repositories, documents, spreadsheets, slides, reports), yet the parsed views they search, the native files they edit, the changes they review, and the artifacts they submit can refer to different versions of the same work product. We formulate this as a workspace-state contract: every view should be explicitly tied to a version of the evolving workspace state. Coding agents partly address this need through repository contracts for search, diffs, and tests, whereas an analogous contract is less explicit for PDFs, spreadsheets, slides, notebooks, and mixed-format project folders. We propose StagedWorkspace, a versioned workspace for knowledge-work agents. The workspace binds parsed records and review diffs to content hashes of the native files as they change. In fixed-harness ablations on OfficeQA Pro and APEX-Agents, dual parsed/native access has the highest point estimate for every tested model; relative to the more limiting single view, it improves OfficeQA Pass@1 by 8.3-12.1 points and APEX mean rubric score by 4.7-9.2 points. SW-AGENT scores 63.9% with Gemini 3.1 Pro on OfficeQA and 42.1 with GPT-5.4 Nano on APEX, compared with published same-model scores of 29.3% and 25.5, respectively. A paired review-axis ablation on 57 file-editing tasks further finds higher observed scores when diffs are visible. These results identify workspace state as an experimental variable in knowledge-work agents and motivate benchmarks that score evidence, staged edits, and submitted artifacts as explicit state transitions.

</details>


### [65] [Delegation Asymmetry in Agentic Recommender Systems: Measuring Two-Sided Receptivity in Online Dating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18058)
*Daria Leshchikova,Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Valerii Klimov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过两项大规模调查（N=2894和N=2617）构建了代理接受度的潜在变量测量模型，发现发送与接收代理通信的意愿是两个可分离的构念，并揭示了部署不对称性：用户更愿意部署自己的代理而非接收他人的代理。在随机配对反事实下，仅4-13%的有向配对能结合部署与参与。设计反事实表明，互惠要求会削减一半以上的交互量，而基于接收接受度路由代理联系可将每次联系参与度提高三倍（AUC=0.88）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM代理在匹配平台上的应用日益普遍，但其可行性取决于用户是否接受代理中介的通信，而这一条件很少被研究。理解用户对代理通信的接受度，对于代理推荐系统的设计至关重要。

Method: 采用潜在变量测量模型，基于分级响应模型和潜在回归，比较不同模型结构以区分发送和接收意愿；通过随机配对反事实和设计反事实分析，验证代理部署与接收的匹配情况及其影响。

Result: 发送和接收意愿是高度相关但可分离的构念（rho=0.92, Delta BIC=52）；部署阈值（-0.38）远低于参与阈值（+0.32或+1.39），部署倾向约为参与倾向的三倍；随机配对下仅4-13%的配对有效；互惠要求减少一半以上交互，而路由代理联系使参与度提高三倍（AUC=0.88）。

Conclusion: 用户对代理通信的接受度存在系统性不对称，设计代理系统时应考虑接收方接受度，采用披露、选择机制和接受度感知的匹配策略，以提升整体互动效果。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents that converse on a user's behalf are an emerging design pattern in matching platforms, yet their viability depends on a condition rarely examined: users must accept not only delegating conversation to an agent, but also receiving agent-mediated communication from others. We study this condition using two large-scale surveys of active users of a major dating platform (N=2,894 on generative profile features; N=2,617 on autonomous conversational agents, fielded in two languages). We develop a latent-variable measurement model of agent receptivity based on graded response models with latent regression, and show via model comparison that willingness to send and willingness to receive agent communication are distinct constructs: highly correlated (rho=0.92) but separable (Delta BIC=52), with partial measurement invariance across languages. The model quantifies a systematic delegation asymmetry: deploying one's own agent requires far lower receptivity (threshold -0.38) than engaging a counterpart's agent (+0.32; full engagement +1.39), and mean deployment propensity exceeds engagement propensity roughly threefold. Under a random-pairing counterfactual derived from stated receptivity, only 4-13% of directed dyads combine agent deployment with receiver engagement, with a pronounced gender-directional imbalance. Design counterfactuals quantify the levers: a reciprocity requirement cuts interaction volume by half or more by excluding nearly two-thirds of would-be deployment, while routing agent contacts on receive receptivity triples per-contact engagement, a lift that survives out-of-sample validation with the target item held out (AUC 0.88, 3.1x quartile lift under respondent-level cross-validation). We discuss implications for agentic recommender design, including disclosure, opt-in mechanics, and receptivity-aware matchmaking.

</details>

### [66] [AerialYield-B2D: A Greenhouse Blueberry Dataset with Five-Stage Ripeness Masks and Fruit Counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16973)
*Iyyakutti Iyappan Ganapathi,Afeefa Azam,Muhammad Owais,Irfan Hussain,Yusra Abdulrahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AerialYield-B2D数据集，包含514张温室蓝莓图像和30,195个实例，涵盖五个成熟阶段，提供分割掩码、计数标签等资源，用于蓝莓成熟度分割和计数研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开温室图像资源缺乏密集的成熟度阶段掩码，限制了蓝莓成熟度评估相关研究的发展。

Method: 构建了AerialYield-B2D数据集，通过智能手机、视频帧和无人机视频帧采集图像，并进行像素级标注，提供多种注释和验证信息。

Result: 数据集包含514张RGB图像和30,195个标注实例，覆盖五个成熟阶段，并提供了丰富的注释和验证信息。

Conclusion: 该数据集为蓝莓成熟度分割、计数和类别不平衡分析提供了可复现的资源，但计数标签表示图像级数量而非产量估计。

Abstract: Blueberry ripeness is judged by berry colour, cluster composition, and the distribution of maturity stages within a plant, however, public green house image resources with dense ripeness-stage masks remain limited. We present AerialYield-B2D, where B2D denotes BlueBerry Dataset, acurated real-image resource containing 514 RGB images and 30,195 annotated blueberry instances across five ripeness stages: green immature, pale pink, pink-turns-purple, fully ripe and over-ripe. The release provides class-specific binary masks, overall berry masks, semantic label maps, image-level count tables, SHA-256 hashes, source metadata, recommended train/validation/test splits and technical validations. AerialYield is the broader project name; this release does not provide harvest weight, fruit mass or per-area yield measurements, and the count labels should therefore be interpreted as image-level berry counts rather than yield estimates. The images include 424 smartphone greenhouse images, 67 video-derived frames, and 23 DJI Fly video-frame samples, providing a reproducible dataset for ripeness segmentation, berry counting, and class-imbalance analysis in controlled-environment blueberry production.

</details>


### [67] [Multi-Observer Vehicle Localization Case Study with Roadside Radar and Connected Vehicle Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16966)
*Aleksi Pippuri,Nilusha Jayawickrama,Risto Ojala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种融合路边雷达和联网车辆LiDAR的多观察者车辆定位框架，实验表明在LiDAR完全可用时融合收益有限，但AEKF仍略有提升，且低更新率下共享仍有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合交通中准确估计车辆位置至关重要，但决策级融合路边基础设施与联网车辆观测的实际证据不足。

Method: 采用两个扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）策略，融合路边雷达和动态LiDAR的物体级检测，并在真实路口数据上测试不同条件。

Result: LiDAR主导融合性能，雷达仅提供有限改进；AEKF小幅优于LiDAR基线，降更新率时观测仍有用。

Conclusion: 决策级融合带来场景相关收益，而非自动优于强单传感器基线；数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: In modern intelligent transportation systems, it is essential to accurately estimate vehicle positions, especially in mixed traffic conditions where both connected and conventional vehicles coexist. Roadside infrastructure and connected vehicles can provide complementary observations of the same traffic scene, but real-world evidence on decision-level fusion between these sources remains limited. This paper proposes a multi-observer vehicle localization framework that fuses compact object-level detections from a static roadside radar and a dynamic LiDAR-equipped connected vehicle. We evaluate the framework with real-world data collected at an urban intersection in Helsinki, Finland, with a separately instrumented target vehicle used as the reference trajectory. Two extended Kalman filter based strategies for the localization task were benchmarked. The performance of the radar and LiDAR sensors were evaluated separately, and the two fusion strategies were explored under nominal sensing conditions, reduced LiDAR update rates, simulated LiDAR occlusions, and different target-vehicle motion states. The results show that, under full LiDAR availability, fusion performance is dominated by the LiDAR observations, while the less accurate and less consistent radar observations provide only limited additional improvement. Nevertheless, AEKF achieves small gains over the LiDAR-only baseline, and object-level connected vehicle observations remain useful when shared at reduced update rates. These findings indicate that decision-level fusion provides scenario-dependent benefits rather than automatic improvement over a strong single-sensor baseline. We release the dataset and implementation on Github to support further research: https://github.com/AppuriAalto/multi-observer-vehicle-tracking

</details>


### [68] [YILDIZ-VPR: A Novel Dataset with Dense Coverage Under Diverse Environmental Conditions for Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17033)
*Serdar Yildiz,Abbas Memiş,Songül Varli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了YILDIZ-VPR，一个在伊尔迪兹技术大学校园内通过重复步行收集的视觉地点识别数据集，包含不同时间、季节和天气条件下的户外场景，并配有GPS及其他传感器数据，为图像和时间序列VPR研究提供资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VPR数据集缺乏从行人视角收集的密集且多样的视觉数据，难以支持在真实户外条件下对VPR方法的研究。

Method: 使用GoPro 9相机在校园内进行重复步行录像，同时同步采集GPS、陀螺仪、速度和温度等传感器数据，并对视频帧进行提取和标注。

Result: 构建了一个包含丰富视觉内容和长期变化（时间、季节、天气）的VPR数据集，覆盖历史建筑、现代建筑、道路、绿地和林区等场景。

Conclusion: YILDIZ-VPR为图像和时间序列视觉地点识别在真实户外条件下的研究提供了有价值的数据资源。

Abstract: Visual Place Recognition (VPR) aims to recognize the location of a query image by comparing it with a set of geo-referenced images. Although many datasets have been proposed for VPR, collecting dense and diverse visual data from pedestrian-level viewpoints is still an important need. In this paper, we introduce YILDIZ-VPR, a visual geo-localization dataset collected through repeated walking traversals on the Davutpasa campus of Yildiz Technical University. The dataset includes outdoor scenes captured at different times of day, seasons, and weather conditions. It contains a wide range of visual content, including historical buildings, modern structures, roads, green areas, and wooded regions. Each video was recorded with a GoPro 9 camera and synchronized with GPS sensor data to provide location labels for the extracted frames. In addition to GPS coordinates, the dataset also includes auxiliary sensor information such as gyroscope, speed, and temperature data. With its dense coverage and long-term visual variability, YILDIZ-VPR provides a useful resource for studying image-based and temporal visual place recognition under realistic outdoor conditions.

</details>


### [69] [PXDepth: Pixel-Space Modeling for Structure Preserving Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16984)
*Zhiyuan Yuan,Guanying Chen,Lingteng Qiu,Ruimao Zhang,Shuguang Cui,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PXDepth，一种结合大patch ViT全局上下文与像素级Transformer的深度估计模型，在不牺牲全局准确性的前提下保留细粒度结构和锐利边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度估计器在零样本泛化中难以保留细粒度结构和物体边界，归因于大patch ViT编码器与卷积解码器的组合，粗粒度token化削弱像素级线索。

Method: PXDepth分离全局上下文建模（大patch ViT）与像素级深度预测（上下文调制像素Transformer块），保持高分辨率空间表征。

Result: 在多个零样本基准上，PXDepth在保持全局深度准确性的同时，有效保留局部几何细节，且推理高效。

Conclusion: PXDepth验证了分离全局与像素级建模的有效性，为深度估计提供了一种兼顾效率与精度的新方案。

Abstract: Recent monocular depth estimators achieve strong zero-shot generalization, yet often struggle to preserve fine-grained structures and object boundaries. We attribute this limitation to the prevalent combination of large-patch ViT encoders and convolutional decoders, as coarse tokenization can weaken pixel-level cues that upsampling cannot fully recover. To address this issue, we propose PXDepth, a discriminative monocular depth model that separates global context modeling from pixel-level depth prediction. Specifically, a large-patch ViT captures global scene context, while a pixel-space predictor composed of Context-Modulated Pixel Transformer blocks maintains high-resolution spatial representations throughout depth estimation. This design preserves fine structures and sharp boundaries without sacrificing global depth consistency. Across diverse zero-shot benchmarks, PXDepth combines faithful local geometry with competitive global depth accuracy while remaining efficient at inference. Our code and model are available at https://yuanzhy29.github.io/PXDepth-Page/.

</details>


### [70] [The 10th AI City Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17044)
*Zheng Tang,Shuo Wang,David C. Anastasiu,Ming-Ching Chang,Anuj Sharma,Quan Kong,Munkhjargal Gochoo,Jun-Wei Hsieh,Tomasz Kornuta,Zhedong Zheng,Renran Tian,Judah Goldfeder,Fulgencio Navarro,Yuxing Wang,Yizhou Wang,Sameer Satish Pusegaonkar,Anqi Li,Nalin Dadhich,Ridham Kachhadiya,Dhanishtha Patil,Haoquan Liang,Jiajun Li,Han Zhang,Yilin Zhao,Zaid Pervaiz Bhat,Shuyu Yang,Ashutosh Kumar,Rong Wang,Rafael Martin Nieto,Peter Christiansen,Ahmed Abduljawad,Mohanrasu Shanmugam,Nadeem Shaik,Sujit Biswas,Xunlei Wu,Vidya Murali,Rama Chellappa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文总结了第十届AI城市挑战赛（与ECCV 2026共同举办）的设置、数据集、评估协议和结果，展示了多摄像头感知、多模态推理等六大赛道，参赛规模从245队增至325队，覆盖26个国家和地区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI城市挑战赛旨在推动智能交通、智慧城市和物理AI领域的社区基准测试发展，从2017年起步，已扩展为涵盖多摄像头感知、多模态推理等任务的综合基准，以应对实际城市环境中的复杂挑战。

Method: 挑战赛设有六个主要赛道，包括多摄像头3D感知、交通安全字幕与VQA、交通异常推理、基于文本的行人异常搜索、生成式交通视频预测和跨城市目标检测。此外，赛道3还扩展了两个域外排行榜（赛道7和8），分别针对鱼眼交通违规理解和行人情境意图VQA。参赛系统结合了基础模型、几何基础、检索或重排序、合成数据设计、域适应和受控推理等技术。

Result: 2026年挑战赛吸引了325支注册团队（2025年为245支），来自26个国家和地区（2025年为15个）。论文总结了各赛道的排行榜结果，成功系统普遍采用基础模型与几何定相结合、检索重排、合成数据设计、域适应等方法。

Conclusion: 挑战赛的持续增长和多样化赛道表明其社区基准价值，成功的系统方法为智能交通和物理AI领域提供了可参考的技术路径，未来有望继续推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: The 10th AI City Challenge, held with ECCV 2026, marks a decade of community benchmarking for intelligent transportation, smart cities, and physical AI. Since its 2017 start with vehicle detection, classification, and tracking, the challenge has grown into a broad benchmark suite for multi-camera perception, multimodal reasoning, synthetic-to-real learning, generative forecasting, and privacy-preserving evaluation. The 2026 edition continued this growth with 325 registered teams, up from 245 in 2025, and participation from 26 countries and regions, up from 15. Its six primary tracks cover multi-camera 3D perception, transportation safety captioning and VQA, traffic anomaly reasoning, text-based person anomaly search, generative traffic video forecasting, and cross-city object detection. Track 3 further includes two out-of-domain leaderboards, submitted as Tracks 7 and 8, for fisheye traffic-violation understanding and pedestrian situated-intent VQA. This paper summarizes the challenge setup, datasets, evaluation protocols, leaderboard results, and workshop papers. Across tracks, successful systems combine foundation models with geometric grounding, retrieval or reranking, synthetic-data design, domain adaptation, and controlled inference.

</details>


### [71] [CAS-FD: Contact-Aware Temporal Sampling for Single-View Foul vs Dive Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17060)
*Md. Jahidul Islam,Mahfujul Alam,Md. Nazmul Islam Seyam,Md. Tamim Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于足球假摔识别的新型接触感知采样方法，在单视角广播视频上显著提升了精度，并发布了平衡数据集与可复现代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球比赛中区分真实犯规与假摔极具挑战性，尤其在仅有单视角广播画面时，缺乏多视角信息，且现有方法未聚焦于接触时刻，导致识别性能不足。

Method: 构建了包含600个片段的平衡单视角Foul/Dive数据集，提出接触感知采样策略，将模型注意力集中在物理接触发生时刻，而非均匀处理所有帧，并结合其他管道组件进行识别。

Result: 在测试集上达到86.0%的准确率和0.860的宏F1，相比不感知接触的方法提升12个百分点，且在未见数据上优势更明显。同时进行了组件级人类标注对比，分析了系统成功与失败的原因。

Conclusion: 接触感知采样有效解决了单视角足球接触事件识别问题，提供了数据集、可复现流程和评估框架，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Distinguishing a genuine foul from a simulated dive in football remains one of the sport's most contested fine-grained recognition problems, especially when such decisions have to be from a single broadcast view without multi-view camera angle. We introduce a balanced 600-clip single-view Foul/Dive dataset and show that contact-aware sampling concentrating the model's attention around the moment of physical contact rather than treating all frames equally yields substantially improved recognition of this contact- specific problem. The proposed approach achieves 86.0% accuracy and macro-F1 0.860 on the held-out test split, a 12 percentage- point gain over contact-unaware alternatives that grows further on unseen data. We also evaluate each pipeline component against human annotations, establishing where and why the system suc- ceeds and fails. The result is a documented dataset, a reproducible single-view pipeline, and a grounded evaluation framework for fine-grained contact-event recognition in broadcast football footage. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/hossain- tamim/contact-aware-dive.

</details>


### [72] [Inference-Time Attention Steering for Vision-Language-Action Driving Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17095)
*Darshan Nagendra Prasad,Lars Ullrich,Knut Graichen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有界的加性预softmax注意力偏置方法，用于在推理时无需重训练即可将VLA驾驶模型的注意力转向安全关键参与者。实验表明，该方法在轨迹输出上呈现单调剂量反应，且效果集中在后期层，但不会影响推理路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA驾驶模型在推理时缺乏直接调整注意力以关注安全关键参与者的机制，通常需要重训练或额外修改。

Method: 在Alpamayo-R1的Qwen3-VL骨干上，对检测器定位的交通参与者的视觉token施加有界加性预softmax注意力偏置，作为前向钩子实现，不修改权重。

Result: 在50个换道场景中，轨迹解码器对偏置幅度呈单调响应，最大平均位移约17cm，横向偏移可达140cm。效果集中在后期层，钩住层数越多效果越强，且轨迹向被关注参与者偏移，但文本推理链不变。

Conclusion: 该方法通过注意力偏置有效引导模型视觉注意力，但不影响推理路径，证明了注意力偏置的暴露验证而非鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) driving models couple a reasoning stage with a diffusion-based trajectory decoder, but do not give a direct way to redirect attention toward safety-critical actors at inference time without retraining. We studied a bounded additive pre-softmax attention bias on the visual tokens of detector localized traffic actors on Alpamayo-R1's Qwen3-VL backbone. It is applied as a fail open forward pre-hook with no weight changes. On 50 lane-change scenarios from the Physical AI World Model Synthetic dataset. The trajectory decoder shows a monotonic dose response in the bias magnitude, separate from a paired zero bias control at every tested magnitude. It reaches $\approx 17$\,cm mean displacement with lateral shifts up to $\sim 140$\ cm at the clamp. A layer ablation places the action-relevant signal in late layers, where the effect increases with the number of hooked layers (2.0cm for the first 8 layers; 67.6cm for all 36). A per call injection audit explains why the Chain-of-Causation text never changes. The mask based bias never reaches the reasoning pathway in this serving stack, so the invariance is verified exposure, not robustness. Steered trajectories tend to shift toward the attended actor, suggesting the bias governs where the model looks rather than encoding a target behavior.

</details>


### [73] [OV3D-Bench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Open-Vocabulary Monocular 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17110)
*Mariia Gladkova,Neehar Peri,Ishan Khatri,Deva Ramanan,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OV3D-Bench基准，用于在真实部署条件下评估开放词汇单目3D检测器，发现它们定位能力强但语义标签易错，且对提示词敏感，并证明使用冻结的闭集检测器加对比视觉-语言编码器可实现有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇单目3D检测器评估协议不一致，依赖部署时不存在的每图像类别Oracle，且将几何与语义混在一个AP指标中，导致无法准确诊断模型的实际能力。

Method: 设计OV3D-Bench基准，采用数据集级类别名提示替代每图像Oracle，沿定位、语义鲁棒性和跨域迁移三个轴解耦评估，并测试了七种代表性检测器。

Result: 检测器定位良好但语义标签常错；性能对提示词高度敏感；目标感知协议会夸大性能；简单重映射闭集检测器输出可媲美专门开放词汇方法。

Conclusion: 几何定位已较成熟，开放词汇语义理解仍是主要瓶颈，且现有评估协议需改进。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary monocular 3D detectors report strong in-domain performance, but each evaluates under a different protocol, several rely on per-image category oracles unavailable at deployment, and all collapse geometry and semantics into a single AP metric. To address this, we introduce OV3D-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark that compares open-vocabulary monocular 3D detectors under deployment-realistic conditions across seven indoor and outdoor datasets. Our benchmark replaces the per-image class name oracle with test-time dataset-level class name prompts, and decouples detection accuracy along three axes: localization, semantic robustness, and cross-domain transfer. We evaluate seven representative detectors and find that (i) they localize objects well yet often mislabel a correctly localized box as a semantically adjacent category; (ii) accuracy is highly sensitive to prompt phrasing (e.g. WildDet3D's performance collapses from 18.6 to 5.4 AP when prompted with "a detailed high-resolution photo of a car" rather than "car"); and (iii) the widely adopted target-aware protocol hides these errors (e.g. inflating DetAny3D's AP by 1.9 $\times$ on ScanNet). Lastly, we demonstrate that simply remapping a frozen closed-vocabulary detector's predictions using a contrastive vision-language encoder such as SigLIPv2 performs competitively against recent purpose-built open-vocabulary methods. This indicates that geometric localization is more mature, while open-vocabulary semantics remains the primary bottleneck.

</details>


### [74] [PROBE: Manipulation-Grounded Visual Question Answering with VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17129)
*Vineet Bhat,Siyi Chen,Alex Zook,Xuning Yang,Stan Birchfield,Valts Blukis,Jonathan Tremblay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出操纵接地视觉问答（MG-VQA）任务，并构建PROBE框架（包含模拟器、基准和微调方法），证明基于代理的工具使用策略优于纯感知，且微调模型可实现模拟到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在静态场景中表现出色，但无法处理真实世界杂乱环境中的动态推理，如机器人需先移动障碍物才能回答被遮挡物体的问题，这需要模型在操作过程中动态更新场景理解。

Method: 开发PROBE-Sim模拟器生成PROBE-Bench基准（150个任务、6种问题类型），评估前沿VLM在感知-操作-回答任务中的表现；提出PROBE-Agent微调配方，利用教师模型生成的成功轨迹蒸馏至较小开源模型，并设计混合数据策略提升操作效率。

Result: 所有前沿VLM的代理工具方法比纯感知基线平均提升8.0%；PROBE-Agent微调模型比现成代理基线平均提升11.5%，并泛化到未见物体和新任务；真实世界部署验证了模拟到现实的迁移。

Conclusion: 操纵接地VQA是VLM在动态场景中的关键挑战，代理工具使用和针对性微调能显著提升性能，且模拟训练可迁移至现实，为机器人交互式推理提供有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language Models (VLMs) excel at 2D grounding, spatial reasoning and agentic tool-based planning in static scenes. However, consider asking a home robot "Is my medication still in the cabinet?" The answer may be physically hidden behind a row of containers that must first be moved aside. Answering such questions in real-world cluttered environments requires reasoning in dynamic scenes: distractors must be manipulated to reveal occluded objects, and each action changes the scene the model must reason over. We formalize this setting as Manipulation-Grounded Visual Question Answering (MG-VQA) and introduce PROBE, a framework for benchmarking and finetuning VLM agents on such tasks.
