Bridging these noise descriptions, this paper proposes a zonotopic mixture noise model, in which the noise is generated by drawing a zonotope from a finite collection according to fixed probabilities and then realizing an arbitrary element of it. For this noise model, we derive the zonotopic mixture filter, which propagates a bank of zonotopic Kalman filters over mode histories, discards the histories falsified by the data, and weights the surviving ones by their relative probability. The resulting state enclosures yield guaranteed coverage probabilities and remain valid for every noise realization compatible with the bounds, and a greedy mixture reduction scheme preserves these statistical guarantees while keeping the representation tractable. Numerical examples illustrate the proposed approach and its potential benefits over related state estimation methods.

</details>


### [257] [Adaptive Model Predictive Control for Ground Vehicles: Review and Demonstrative Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17902)
*Chetana Gadgil,Mahendra Singh Tomar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文综述了自适应模型预测控制（AMPC）在自动驾驶车辆中的应用，涵盖多种AMPC方法，并通过仿真演示了权重与速度自适应在轨迹跟踪中的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MPC在面对模型不准确、计算负担和动态环境时存在局限，需要开发能够实时调整控制策略的AMPC方法，以提升自动驾驶车辆的鲁棒性和适应性。

Method: 系统回顾了增益调度MPC、在线模型估计MPC、权重自适应MPC、预测时域自适应MPC、基于学习的MPC以及混合MPC等文献，并进行了自适应MPC控制器的仿真演示。

Result: 综述了各类AMPC方法及其优缺点，仿真结果展示了提出的自适应MPC在轨迹跟踪中的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: AMPC是应对自动驾驶车辆动态不确定性的重要方法，未来应进一步结合学习与优化，提高实时性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper reviews Adaptive Model Predictive Control (AMPC) methods for Autonomous Vehicles (AVs), focusing on control strategies that dynamically adapt to uncertainties and changing conditions in real-time. The critical role of Adaptive Model Predictive Control (AMPC) in addressing the challenges of autonomous vehicle control are discussed. For the scope of this paper, AMPC is defined as a class of Model Predictive Control (MPC) techniques that modify the system model, cost function, constraints, or prediction horizon, based on real-time data. Traditional MPC, while effective for constrained optimization, struggles with model inaccuracies, computational demands, and dynamic environments, necessitating AMPC methods. The review covers existing literature on Gain scheduled MPC, Online Model Estimation MPC, Weight Adaptive MPC, Horizon Adaptive MPC, Learning Based MPC, and Hybrid MPC that combines MPC with other control methods. In addition to the survey, a demonstrative simulation of an adaptive MPC controller is presented that illustrates practical aspects of weight and speed adaptation in trajectory tracking.

</details>


### [258] [Steady-State Equivalent Circuit Model for Data Center Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17925)
*Muhammad Hamza Ali,Peng Sang,Hyeon Woo,Hyein Kang,Sungyun Choi,Amritanshu Pandey*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出数据中心稳态等效电路模型，显式建模IT负载、电源转换及冷却系统，揭示异构工作负载对电网影响，相比传统恒定PQ模型能更准确预测线路过载风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚合模型忽略IT负载与电网间的电气关系、功率转换损耗及异构分布，导致规划研究不准确。

Method: 构建包含IT负载、电源单元、冷却和辅助系统的等效电路模型，结合利用率相关服务器功率模型与损耗感知电源模型，并兼容潮流计算。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真显示，相比固定效率PQ模型，新模型预测最紧张线路在30%样本中超限；同质利用率高估线路负载变异性17%-46%。

Conclusion: 所提ECM能更准确反映数据中心对电网的稳态影响，强调异构负载建模的重要性，为电力规划提供更可靠工具。

Abstract: Planners currently represent data centers as aggregate constant-PQ or ZIP loads in steady-state interconnection and contingency studies. These aggregate models are computationally convenient. However, they obscure the electrical relationship between computational workloads, server utilization, and grid-side demand. They ignore the internal power-electronic conversion stages of IT loads and assume homogeneous workload distributions across the compute clusters. This hides operating-point-dependent converter losses and efficiency variations. We propose a steady-state equivalent-circuit model (ECM) for data centers, which explicitly builds circuit models for IT loads, power supply units, cooling, and auxiliary systems. For power supply units, the equivalent circuit model explicitly represents internal power-electronic conversion stages. For IT loads, we develop a utilization-dependent server power model, and we combine it with loss-aware ECMs of power supply units. This approach captures the grid-side impact of heterogeneous workload distributions while preserving compatibility with conventional power-flow analysis. We evaluate this data center ECM in large-scale transmission power flows, using Monte Carlo simulations under heterogeneous and homogeneous cluster utilization. In comparison with the fixed-efficiency constant-PQ model, the ECM predicts that the most stressed line exceeds its thermal limit in about 30% of Monte Carlo samples. The results further show that homogeneous server utilization overstates line-loading variability by 17%-46% relative to heterogeneous server utilization, depending on the intra-cluster workload correlation.

</details>


### [259] [A Coalitional Game for Demand-Side Management in a Micro-Grid with Multiple Electricity Retailers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17934)
*Pablo R Baldivieso-Monasterios,Fernando Genis Mendoza,George Konstantopoulos,Dario Bauso*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多零售商电力网络的需求侧管理框架，利用联盟博弈和混合整数优化来联合优化零售价格、用户需求和网络分区，并通过多目标优化算法找到平衡零售商利润和消费者福利的联盟结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有需求侧管理研究多关注单一零售商或集中式优化，而实际电力市场存在多个竞争零售商，需要分析它们之间的竞争与合作关系，以及风险对定价的影响。

Method: 将零售商交互建模为联盟博弈，产生耦合的混合整数优化问题；设计基于多目标优化的联盟形成算法，并扩展至条件风险价值（CVaR）的风险共享公式；证明算法有限步收敛并恢复弱帕累托有效解。

Result: 算法在有限步内收敛，并能得到包含多个可接受权衡的均衡分区集合；数值模拟验证了方法在学术案例中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为多零售商需求侧管理中的竞争、联盟形成和风险感知定价提供了易处理的求解方法，有助于分析不同目标间的权衡。

Abstract: This paper develops a demand-side management framework for electricity networks with multiple competing retailers. The interaction among retailers is formulated as a coalitional game, yielding a family of coupled mixed-integer optimisation problems in which retail prices, consumer power demands, and the network partition are jointly optimised. To solve this problem, we propose a coalition-formation algorithm based on multi-objective optimisation principles. The algorithm seeks to identify coalition structures that balance retailer profit and consumer welfare. We prove that the proposed algorithm converges in a finite number of steps and recovers a subset of weakly Pareto-efficient solutions of the coupled optimisation problems. The framework is further extended to a risk-sharing formulation, in which the objective is defined using conditional value-at-risk. Numerical simulations on an academic example demonstrate the method's behaviour and show that the resulting equilibrium partition set contains several admissible trade-offs between the competing objectives. The results provide a tractable approach for analysing competition, coalition formation, and risk-aware pricing in multi-retailer demand-side management systems.

</details>


### [260] [Infinite-Horizon Inverse Linear-Quadratic Differential Games with State- and Control-Dependent Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17939)
*Lucas Günther,Karl Handwerker,Felix Thömmes,Balint Varga,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种求解带状态与控制相关噪声的N人无限时域线性二次（LQ）微分博弈逆问题的方法，通过推导线性反馈纳什均衡的充要条件，并利用核表示来刻画与观测轨迹一致的成本参数集合，最终通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性并揭示了逆问题的固有模糊性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注LQ博弈的正问题（给定成本参数求均衡），而逆问题（从观测轨迹反推成本参数）在实际中具有重要意义，尤其是在随机噪声环境下尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 推导了随机LQ微分博弈中线性反馈纳什均衡的充要条件（耦合随机代数Riccati方程），并进一步推导了这些方程的核表示，以显式刻画所有与观测均衡轨迹一致的成本参数组合。

Result: 数值结果验证了所提方法的有效性，并确认了理论发现，同时突出了逆问题固有的非唯一性（歧义性）。

Conclusion: 该研究为随机LQ微分博弈的逆问题提供了理论框架和求解方法，证明了逆问题存在本质上的模糊性，即多个成本参数组合可能产生相同的均衡轨迹。

Abstract: This paper presents a method to solve the inverse problem for N-player infinite-horizon linear-quadratic (LQ) differential games with state- and control-dependent noise. For this stochastic setting, we derive necessary and sufficient conditions for linear feedback Nash equilibria, which take the form of coupled stochastic algebraic Riccati equations. We then derive a kernel representation of these equations to explicitly characterize the set of all cost function parameter combinations across players that are consistent with observed equilibrium trajectories, thereby solving the associated inverse problem. Numerical results illustrate the approach and confirm the theoretical findings, highlighting the inherent ambiguity of the inverse problem.

</details>


### [261] [Policy Iteration for Linear-Quadratic Stochastic Differential Games with State- and Control-Dependent Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17940)
*Karl Handwerker,Felix Thömmes,Lucas Günther,Balint Varga,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对状态和控制依赖噪声的随机微分博弈的新型顺序策略迭代（PI）方法，该方法保持了均方稳定性，并导出了Nash均衡处PI映射的Fréchet导数的闭式表达式，从而揭示了局部误差传播机制和线性收敛条件。同时，提出了一种基于同伦的初始化方法，确保有效起始点，并通过数值示例验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略迭代在随机微分博弈中面临两大挑战：迭代过程中可能失去稳定性，以及难以找到初始稳定化策略。本文旨在解决这些问题，并深入理解局部收敛行为。

Method: 提出一种顺序策略迭代方法，更新过程中保持均方稳定性；推导PI映射在Nash均衡处的Fréchet导数闭式表达式，以分析局部误差传播；提出同伦初始化方法，确保有效起始点，并给出局部线性收敛的充分条件。

Result: 推导了Fréchet导数闭式表达式，明确了控制依赖噪声、策略评估敏感性和更新顺序对局部误差传播的影响，并给出了局部线性收敛的充分条件。通过数值示例验证了算法和理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的顺序策略迭代方法结合同伦初始化，能有效求解随机微分博弈，且具有良好的稳定性和收敛性，为相关领域提供了理论依据和实用算法。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel sequential policy iteration (PI) method for stochastic differential games with state- and control-dependent noise. The updates preserve mean-square stability, so that the iteration is well posed. We further derive a closed-form expression for the Fréchet derivative of the sequential PI map at a Nash equilibrium. The resulting characterization reveals how control-dependent noise, policy-evaluation sensitivity, and update ordering govern local error propagation, and yields explicit sufficient conditions for local linear convergence. Since finding an initial stabilizing solution is a major challenge in policy iteration, we also propose a homotopy-based initialization that ensures a valid starting point. The effectiveness of the proposed PI algorithm and the analytical results are verified through a numerical example.

</details>


### [262] [The geometric Laplace transform: Definition, existence and properties of the Geometric Algebra Laplace transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18043)
*Manel Velasco,Arnau Dòria-Cerezo,Isiah Zaplana*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了几何代数框架下的拉普拉斯变换定义，适用于签名不超过5的几何代数，为动态系统（如电路）的建模与分析提供新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究开始将几何代数应用于动态系统（如电路）的建模与控制，而将微分方程转换到拉普拉斯域需要几何代数中的拉普拉斯变换定义，现有工作仅扩展到部分超复数代数，需进一步推广。

Method: 在几何代数中引入拉普拉斯变换的定义，并推导其性质，确保适用于签名不超过5的几何代数。

Result: 成功定义了适用于签名≤5的几何代数的拉普拉斯变换及其性质。

Conclusion: 该定义为几何代数在动态系统分析中的应用提供了基础，尤其对电路建模与控制有重要意义。

Abstract: Recent publications have started to explore the application of Geometric Algebra (GA) to the modeling, analysis and control of dynamical systems and, in particular, electrical circuits. Since a crucial element there is to transform the ordinary differential equations governing the dynamical system which models the systems' behavior from the real domain to the Laplace domain, a definition of the Laplace transform in GA is needed. In the present work, we extend previous works dealing with extension to some hiper-complex algebras by introducing a definition of the Laplace transform within the framework of Geometric Algebra (GA). In particular, our definition and its properties are applicable to geometric algebras with signature lower or equal than 5.

</details>


### [263] [Extending and Unifying the Fundamental Tasks of Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18060)
*Dylan Hirsch,William Sharpless,Donggun Lee,Sylvia Herbert*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了广义到达-避免（GRA）任务，它扩展并统一了汉密尔顿-雅可比可达性（HJR）中的经典任务，并展示了其在理论（PDE表示）和实际（组合任务分解）中的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HJR中的经典任务（如到达、避免）形式各异，缺乏统一框架，且难以处理复合任务。

Method: 通过定义GRA任务，证明其包含并扩展经典HJR任务，并利用其值函数分解复合任务（如时序逻辑任务）；从PDE角度分析其表示能力。

Result: GRA严格扩展了HJR可解任务，能分解复合任务的值函数，并可表示HJR-PDE的所有正则解。

Conclusion: GRA是HJR框架中具有理论和实用价值的基本原语。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce the generalized reach-avoid (GRA) task, which both extends and unifies the canonical tasks of Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability (HJR). We show that the GRA not only serves as a common primitive in this class of fundamental tasks, but also strictly extends the fundamental tasks that can be solved with HJR. Moreover, the GRA formulation enables one to compute the value functions of certain composite tasks, including ones from timed temporal logic, by decomposing the value function of the composite task into value functions of GRA tasks. We additionally show that the GRA is also a natural primitive to consider from a PDE perspective, as it can be used to represent all sufficiently regular solutions of the HJ-PDE that is canonical to HJR. Collectively, the results in this work show the theoretical and practical utility of this task within the increasingly important framework of HJR.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [ORViT-DR: Ordinally-Robust Hybrid ViT for Low-Resolution Diabetic Retinopathy Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16958)
*Soumit Kumar Kundu,Nabil Ashab,Bidhan Biswas,Shahadat Hossain Sohag,Saif Mahmud Parvez,Souvik Kumar Kundu,Zunayed Ahmed Rafi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出ORViT-DR混合深度学习框架，结合卷积与Transformer，在低分辨率视网膜图像上实现DR分级，准确率57%，QWK 0.5963。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DR分级具有序数性质，现有方法在低分辨率图像上性能有限，需更可靠的自动化分级系统。

Method: 使用预训练的ViT-Hybrid骨干（BiT-ResNetv2 + Vision Transformer），结合渐进层解冻、层间学习率衰减、EMA和集成预测。

Result: 在RetinaMNIST测试集上达到57.00%准确率、0.5963 QWK、0.4293 macro-F1。

Conclusion: 混合CNN-Transformer架构能有效建模序数视网膜图像特征，为DR分级提供可行方案。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy (DR) is one of the main causes of impaired vision. A good and reliable automated grading system can make the screening process safer and more accurate. Because DR stages progress gradually, the task of grading disease severity naturally follows an ordinal structure in which neighboring classes share similar visual characteristics. In this study, ORViT-DR, a hybrid deep learning framework, is designed to improve DR grading from low-resolution retinal images. The proposed approach combines convolutional feature extraction with transformer-based global context modeling through a pre-trained ViT-Hybrid backbone, which integrates BiT-ResNetv2 with a Vision Transformer architecture. The approach is tested on the RetinaMNIST subset of the MedMNISTv2 dataset, which contains 28x28 retinal fundus images annotated with five levels of disease severity. To promote stable training and better feature learning, the training strategy applies progressive layer unfreezing, layer-wise learning rate decay, exponential moving average (EMA) parameter updates, and ensemble-based prediction during inference. Experimental results on the official RetinaMNIST test set show that the proposed method achieves 57.00% classification accuracy, along with a quadratic weighted kappa score of 0.5963 and a macro-F1 score of 0.4293. These results suggest that hybrid CNN-Transformer architectures can provide effective representations for ordinal retinal image analysis.

</details>


### [265] [MagViT: Interpretable Multi-Magnification Transformers with Patient-Level Model Selection for Breast Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16959)
*Nabil Ashab,Soumit Kumar Kundu,Saif Mahmud Parvez,Shahadat Hossain Sohag,Bidhan Biswas,Nazmus Subha*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的多放大倍数Transformer框架MagViT，用于乳腺组织病理图像分类，在BreakHis数据集上取得了良好性能，并初步验证了跨数据集的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺组织病理图像分类需要处理多尺度组织形态和跨域泛化问题，但现有方法在这两方面仍有挑战。

Method: 采用ViT骨干网络提取不同放大倍数（40X-400X）的特征，通过可学习的门控机制融合多尺度表示，并基于患者级五折交叉验证选择最优分支。

Result: 在BreakHis上达到图像准确率0.9191、患者准确率0.9643、宏F1为0.9042；在BUSI和IDC数据集上展示了跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: MagViT通过多尺度融合和患者级选择实现了较好的准确性和可解释性，为乳腺病理图像分类提供了可靠的跨域方案。

Abstract: Breast cancer is one of the most common types of cancer among women around the world. Rapid detection and early treatment can hinder its progress to more complex stages and can impede its spread to other parts of the body. Histopathological image classification is the most common task in cancer detection due to its robustness in analyzing cellular data. Breast histopathology classification requires handling both multi-scale tissue morphology and clinically relevant generalization beyond the source domain. This paper presents MagViT, an interpretable multi-magnification transformer framework with scale-gated fusion and patient-level model selection. The model uses four BreakHis magnifications (40X, 100X, 200X, 400X) and extracts per-scale representations with a ViT backbone, and combines them via a learnable gate that masks missing scales. Patient-level five-fold cross-validation with a fixed seed has been run and compared with three architectural branches. The most accurate branch is then selected as the final model due to the strongest patient-level accuracy while retaining the simplest fusion pathway. On BreakHis, our architecture achieves a mean image accuracy of 0.9191, a mean patient accuracy of 0.9643, and a mean macro-F1 of 0.9042. External transfer experiments provide preliminary evidence of cross-dataset generalization under controlled adaptation settings on BUSI (image accuracy 0.8306, macro-F1 0.7480, patient accuracy 0.8291) and IDC (image accuracy 0.8577, macro-F1 0.8191, patient accuracy 0.8372). Grad-CAM visualization indicates that the model focuses on diagnostically significant and meaningful regions across magnifications. Relative to prior ViT-centered BreakHis work, this study emphasizes patient-level selection and cross-dataset robustness under a reproducible protocol.

</details>


### [266] [Clinical Pathways Matter for Multimodal Deep Learning in Early Alzheimers Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16962)
*Yao Lu,Solveig Kristina Hammonds,Alvaro Fernandez-Quilez*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SigLIP的零样本多模态框架，结合结构MRI和常规临床变量（如MMSE、年龄、性别），对临床前或轻度认知障碍个体进行4年内阿尔茨海默病风险分层，无需任务特定训练，在ADNI队列中达到AUC 0.91，优于CSF和MMSE基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期AD风险识别困难，现有深度学习多模态模型需要任务特定训练且依赖非临床常规生物标志物，缺乏实用性和可扩展性。

Method: 采用SigLIP模型（无需微调）分别提取结构MRI和临床文本嵌入，融合为多模态表示，用于个体级AD风险预测；比较单次访问和两次访问设置。

Result: 单次访问中，MRI+MMSE+年龄+性别的AUC为0.91±0.02，优于CSF Aβ42模型（0.73±0.08）和MMSE模型（0.85±0.22）；两次访问中性能保持或提升。

Conclusion: 零样本多模态融合结构MRI和常规临床变量可为早期AD风险分层提供实用且可扩展的策略，无需任务特定重训练。

Abstract: Identifying individuals at risk of Alzheimer's disease (AD), particularly in the preclinical and early stages, remains challenging. Although deep learning approaches based on structural MRI show promise as a non-invasive biomarker, existing multimodal models require task-specific training and depend on biomarkers that are not routinely available in clinical practice. Here, we propose a zero-shot multimodal framework based on SigLIP that combines structural MRI embeddings with text embeddings of routinely collected clinical variables for early AD risk stratification in individuals at preclinical or mild cognitive impairment (MCI) stages. We evaluated the approach in 416 individuals from the ADNI cohort (age: 72.73 +- 6.7). SigLIP was used without fine-tuning to extract MRI and clinical text embeddings, which were combined into multimodal representations for individual-level AD risk prediction within 4 years. We further compared the model performance in a single-visit and two-visit settings to assess the value of longitudinal information and framework scalability. In the single-visit setting, combining MRI embeddings with MMSE, age, and sex achieved an AUC of 0.91 +- 0.02, outperforming both a CSF A\b{eta}42-based model (AUC 0.73 +- 0.08) and an MMSE-based model (AUC 0.85 +- 0.22). In the two-visit setting, performance was maintained or improved, supporting the scalability of the approach to longitudinal data. These findings suggest that zero-shot multimodal fusion of structural MRI and routinely collected clinical variables may provide a practical and scalable strategy for early AD risk stratification without task-specific retraining.

</details>


### [267] [Technically Plausible but Clinically Misleading? Expert Evaluation of Patient-Personalized Synthetic Prostate MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16964)
*Gabriel Paulo Maglalang Israel,Sol Gedde,Alvaro Fernandez-Quilez*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于临床变量（年龄和PSA）个性化生成前列腺MRI的可行性，并通过专家研究评估其临床合理性。结果显示，个性化合成图像在技术上可行，但专家对其解读信心和误导风险存在差异，提示在临床整合前需谨慎评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI采集成本高、耗时，是前列腺癌诊疗流程的瓶颈。个性化MRI合成有望减少对扫描的依赖，但需验证其临床可接受性和对专家解读的影响。

Method: 使用3D扩散模型，以年龄和PSA为条件生成个性化前列腺MRI；通过图像相似性指标进行技术验证，并开展单盲随机专家研究，采用配对评估和Likert量表评分临床合理性、信心及误导风险。

Result: 个性化合成图像在技术指标上与真实MRI相当，但专家对个性化图像的信心评分变化较大，且存在潜在误导线索，尤其当嵌入临床数据时。

Conclusion: 个性化MRI合成技术上可行，但需进一步研究生成线索如何影响临床解读，以确保安全整合到临床工作流。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is central to prostate cancer assessment, yet its acquisition is costly and time-consuming, making it a major bottleneck in the patient care pathway. Personalized MRI synthesis is a promising direction because it may allow the generation of clinically realistic images tailored to individual patients while reducing the dependence on scanner-acquired imaging. In this work, we in-vestigate whether patient-personalized MRI synthesis produces images that experts perceive as clinically plausible and how such personaliza-tion influences expert interpretation. We synthesize patient-personalized prostate MRI using a 3D diffusion model conditioned on routine pre-imaging clinical variables age and PSA. Using a large publicly available dataset, we first verify that synthesized images are technically compara-ble to scanner-acquired MRIs using standard image similarity metrics. We then conduct a pilot, single-blinded, randomized expert study on a subset of cases sampled to reflect variation in patient clinical pro-files. A radiologist compared personalized and unconditioned synthetic MRIs using paired assessments and Likert-scale ratings of clinical plausibility, confidence, and risk of misleading interpretation, supplemented by qualitative feedback. Although personalized synthetic MRIs appeared technically plausible, expert interpretation highlighted variability in con-fidence and potential risks of misleading cues when routine clinical data was embedded into image generation. These findings suggest that while personalized synthesis may be technically feasible, careful assessment is needed to understand how generated cues influence clinician interpreta-tion before such systems can be safely integrated into clinical workflows.

</details>


### [268] [Harnessing Magnitude-Only and Complex Measurements for Improved Dynamic MRI Reconstruction with Learned Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18036)
*Mahdi Saberi,Yaşar Utku Alçalar,Merve Gülle,Chetan Shenoy,Mehmet Akçakaya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用k空间幅度信息的MRI重建方法（C+Mag），通过ADMM展开框架和幅度感知的数据保真度公式，在动态MRI重建中显著提升了图像质量和相位信息保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MRI重建主要依赖复数测量，而稀疏相位检索中的幅度信息未被充分利用。作者观察到动态MRI中k空间幅度在时间帧间具有强一致性，因此希望利用这一先验信息加速重建，同时不增加额外扫描时间。

Method: 提出C+Mag方法，基于ADMM展开框架，引入幅度感知的数据保真度项，并通过二次平滑和动量更新处理幅度约束的非可微性和非凸性。

Result: 在回顾性欠采样电影MRI和相位对比血流MRI，以及前瞻性欠采样实时电影MRI实验中，相比传统PD-DL方法，该方法在伪影抑制、解剖结构恢复和相位信息保留方面均有改进，并通过盲法专家评估得到支持。

Conclusion: 利用k空间幅度信息可以显著提升加速动态MRI重建质量，且无需额外扫描时间，为MRI重建提供了新的可行方向。

Abstract: MRI reconstruction methods for undersampled k-space data naturally utilize complex-valued measurements. Parallel developments in sparse phase retrieval have shown that magnitude-only measurements may provide complementary information for signal recovery. However, their use in MRI reconstruction remains largely unexplored, due to lack of practical settings where informative magnitude measurements can be obtained without additional scan time. In this work, we investigate the use of auxiliary k-space magnitude information for accelerated steady-state dynamic MRI reconstruction, and demonstrate strong consistency of k-space magnitudes across time-frames. Building on this observation, we propose $\mathbb{C}+\text{Mag}$, a magnitude-informed physics-driven deep learning reconstruction method. The proposed method employs an ADMM-based unrolling framework with a novel magnitude-aware data-fidelity formulation, where quadratically smoothed optimization and momentum-based updates are introduced to address the non-differentiability and non-convexity of the magnitude constraints. Experiments on retrospectively undersampled cine MRI and phase-contrast flow MRI datasets, as well as prospectively undersampled real-time cine MRI acquisitions, demonstrate improved artifact suppression, sharper anatomical recovery, and better preservation of phase information compared to conventional PD-DL methods, which is further supported through blinded expert reader evaluations.

</details>


### [269] [Primitive Representation Learning for Unsupervised Dynamic Contrast Enhanced MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18055)
*Veronika Spieker,Wenqi Huang,Cemre Ariyurek,Liam Timms,Daniel Rueckert,Onur Afacan,Julia A. Schnabel,Sila Kurugol*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于原始图像的多维动态对比增强MRI重建框架，将解剖、动态增强和运动分解为独立时间基函数，实现高质量重建且无需大型训练集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯和高斯伯原始图像的重建方法未考虑动态对比维度，且需要高质量时空重建和高欠采样率下的可靠性。

Method: 设计多维度原始图像框架，将解剖、动态增强和残余运动分解为独立时间基函数，采用模块化分层结构扩展。

Result: 在重建质量和主动脉/肾脏增强曲线准确性上与常规方法竞争，支持更高加速率。

Conclusion: 该框架有效且可扩展，代码已开源，有望用于动态对比增强MRI的可靠定量分析。

Abstract: Reliable quantitative analysis of dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI requires high-quality spatiotemporal reconstructions at high undersampling rates. Scan-specific reconstructions using Gaussian and Gabor primitives have shown promising results without the need for large training datasets, but have not addressed the additional dimension of dynamic contrast. We propose a multi-dimensional, primitive based framework for dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI reconstruction that disentangles the underlying anatomy, the dynamic contrast enhancement, and residual motion into separate temporal basis functions, thereby enabling a geometrical interpretation of the representation. We show that this architecture achieves performance competitive with conventional reconstruction methods, both in reconstruction quality and in the accuracy of extracted aorta and kidney enhancement curves. The modular tier design extends naturally to additional dynamic factors and higher acceleration rates. Code available at https://github.com/compai-lab/ 2026-GaborDCE-spieker.

</details>
